> 🟢 **SLICE MT-1 — TENANT-KEYED BUSINESS-CONFIG CORE (READY_TO_COMMIT — NOT PUSHED, no CI minutes used, 2026-05-22):** First slice of the multi-tenant-store prerequisite that unblocks Section 7 customer-facing off-site surfaces + Customer 2 readiness. **Preflight finding (brutally honest):** the prerequisite was ~80% already shipped — middleware already injects `x-beacon-tenant` per request (Phase 7.4), `connector-store` is already tenant-scoped (composite PK + `currentTenantId()`), OAuth token writes are already tenant-scoped. The ONLY genuine process-global blocker is `src/lib/business-config.ts` — it cached the resolved config in a single module-level `_cached` singleton, so the first request's tenant config poisons every later request in the same lambda. (The off-site loader comment + catalog row claiming connector-store is process-global are STALE — fold the correction into MT-4.) **MT-1 makes business-config tenant-aware with ZERO Ritz behavior change and ZERO consumer migration.** **NO consumer edits · NO Section 7 customer surfaces · NO connector-store / middleware / OAuth / tenant-context changes · NO Supabase table · NO migration · NO production mutation · NO LLM · NO connector/API calls · NO env changes.** **Files (1 src + 1 test extended + 1 NEW invariant + docs)**: (1) MODIFIED `src/lib/business-config.ts` — removed `let _cached` singleton; added tenant-keyed `_cacheByTenant = new Map<string, BusinessConfig>()` + `_placeholderWarnedTenants = new Set<string>()`; `getBusinessConfig(tenantId: string)` overload with per-tenant resolution order **(a)** `BEACON_BUSINESS_CONFIG_JSON_BY_TENANT[tenantId]` → **(b)** when `tenantId === BEACON_TENANT_ID` today's chain EXACTLY (`BEACON_BUSINESS_CONFIG_JSON` → `.data/business-config.json` → `.data/global/business-config.json`; keeps Ritz byte-identical) → **(c)** `.data/tenants/<tenantId>/business-config.json` → **(d)** placeholder; `getBusinessConfigForCurrentTenant()` async wrapper (`getBusinessConfig(await currentTenantId())`); DEPRECATED no-arg `getBusinessConfig()` overload (resolves `BEACON_TENANT_ID` sync, fail-loud if unset, keeps all ~60 consumers green); `saveBusinessConfig(tenantId, patch)` overload (cache-only for non-env tenants; env tenant still writes the shared file = today's behavior); `__resetBusinessConfigCacheForTests()` clears the Map + Set. Pure helpers (`getLocationRegex`/`getServiceRegex`/`isDirectoryDomain`/`getSectionAnalyzerConfig`/`getFaqTemplates`) UNCHANGED (still `config?` with no-arg fallback; tightened in MT-3). No circular import (verified `json-store`/`tenants/store` don't import business-config). > 🟡 **SLICE — BRAND ASSERTION KEY ALIGNMENT (READY_TO_COMMIT — NOT PUSHED, no CI minutes used, 2026-05-22):** Generic tenant-identity fix surfaced by B.4a's diagnostic (Brand-supported = 0 despite Ritz having an `architect-led design-build` `process` assertion). **Root cause:** `TENANT_ASSERTIONS` + `TENANT_NAME_STYLES` in `src/domains/recommendations/brand-assertions.ts` were keyed by tenant **slug** (`"ritz-builders"`), but every production caller passes the tenant **id** (`"tenant-ritz-founder"` — what `currentTenantId()` returns). The slug-based unit test stayed green, masking it. **Fix (Option 4 — re-key by tenantId, no Ritz-specific logic, no async resolver, no caller changes):** re-keyed both registries from slug → canonical id (`tenant-ritz-founder`); updated doc comments to "keyed by `BeaconTenant.id` / `currentTenantId()`, never slug." **This RE-ACTIVATES dormant-but-intended behavior** (LLM allowed-phrase guidance + validator brand-name-style gate for Ritz) — verified correct via the full validator/LLM suite. **NO production mutations · NO migrations · NO LLM calls · NO polls/scans · NO env changes · NO validator-logic changes (only correct data lookup) · NO customer-route changes · NO scanner changes · NO Ritz-specific if-statements.** **Files (1 src + 3 tests + 1 NEW invariant + 5 docs)**: (1) MODIFIED `src/domains/recommendations/brand-assertions.ts` — re-keyed `TENANT_ASSERTIONS` + `TENANT_NAME_STYLES` to `tenant-ritz-founder`; added test-only `__brandRegistryKeys` export (`Object.keys` of both registries) for the new invariant; doc comments updated. Signatures stay sync; assertion phrase/category content UNCHANGED. (2) MODIFIED `src/domains/recommendations/brand-assertions.test.ts` — re-keyed all lookups to `RITZ_TENANT_ID`; +2 regression tests (slug `ritz-builders` now returns `[]` / `null`, no alias). 55 cases. (4) MODIFIED `src/domains/recommendations/specific-edit-validator-faq-pairing.test.ts` (operator-approved) — 1 packet fixture re-keyed; the "rejects 'Ritz' short form in faq_answer" test now fires. `static-bundle.test.ts` left untouched (its 5 slug fixtures don't assert brand-name-style; 22 cases stay green). (5) NEW `tests/architecture/brand-assertions-tenant-key-contract.test.ts` (~130 lines, 7+ cases) — pins every registry key is an `ops/active-tenants.json` tenantId, NO key equals a slug, + behavioral regression (tenantId lookup non-empty / slug lookup empty). **This is the invariant that would have caught the bug.** **Locked decisions honored**: Option 4 (re-key by tenantId); generic (no Ritz `if`); sync signatures preserved; no slug alias (the key-contract invariant forbids slug keys); fixtures corrected (not weakened — same intent: reject bare Ritz, require full first mention). **Re-activation acknowledged + verified**: validator brand-claims (43) + faq-pairing + specific-edit-evidence + recommendation-triggers + B.4a safety-audit all green. **Auto-pass invariants**: B.1 `recommendation-why-render-guard`; `catalog-sync` (1 new row). **Hard contracts honored**: no production mutation · no migration · no LLM · no polls/scans · no env · no validator-logic change · no customer-route change · no scanner change · no Ritz-specific logic. **Quality gates (LOCAL ONLY — no CI minutes used)**: typecheck (clean ✅) · brand-assertions 55 + new invariant 7 = 62 ✅ · validator brand-claims + faq-pairing 43 ✅ · static-bundle 22 ✅ · catalog-sync 8 ✅ · full architecture + full vitest + tenant-env build (TO RUN). **READY_TO_COMMIT — not pushed.** Proposed commit message: `fix(recommendations): key brand assertions by tenant id`. **After deploy, `/diagnostics/recommendation-safety-audit` should flip Brand-supported from 0 to > 0 with architect-led tagged supported (Ritz's `architect-led design-build` process assertion now resolves).** **Next phase**: operator review → local commit only → push → re-inspect the diagnostic to confirm Brand-supported > 0 → then resume the B.4b/B.4c scope decision against correctly-resolved data.

> 🟢 **SLICE 4.5.G-B.4a — GENERIC CLAIM-RISK CLASSIFICATION (TAG, DON'T SUPPRESS) (READY_TO_COMMIT — NOT PUSHED, no CI minutes used, 2026-05-22):** First slice of Section 4.5.G-B.4 (architect_overclaim + unsupported_claim handling). **Operator-locked architecture: GLOBAL risk-pattern detection (in the pure scanner) + TENANT-PROVIDED brand-assertion support (passed via context) + TAG, DON'T SUPPRESS.** Preflight finding: the 40 architect_overclaim + 14 unsupported_claim audit "debt" is largely false-positive — the scanner flat-token-matched with NO brand-assertion carve-out, so it flagged legitimately-asserted phrases (e.g. an `architect-led design-build` `process` assertion). B.4a makes the scanner CLASSIFY each `architect_overclaim` / `unsupported_claim` violation as `brand_supported: true | false` against tenant-supplied assertions — **never suppressing or removing a violation** — and surfaces a per-field × per-term × brand-support debt breakdown on the operator diagnostic so the operator can separate genuine debt from noise BEFORE any guard/validator change. **Tenant-agnostic: the scanner owns a GLOBAL claim-risk registry (token → one of 6 categories: professional_credential / process / ranking / award / guarantee_outcome / superiority) + a GLOBAL unlock map (risk-category → assertion-category strings); it NEVER imports brand-assertions, NEVER imports getBrandAssertions, NEVER imports business-config, NEVER references getRepository, NEVER branches on a tenant name.** The diagnostic page resolves `getBrandAssertions(tenantId)` at the boundary and passes a LOCAL STRUCTURAL shape (`{ id?, phrase, category }`) into the scanner via `context.brandAssertions`. No assertions → `brand_supported` defaults false (safe; preserves pre-B.4a behavior). **Operator-diagnostic + audit-classification ONLY. NO customer surface change · NO migration · NO backfill · NO production mutation · NO LLM · NO scans · NO B.1 why-display-guard change · NO B.2 copy-sanitize change · NO validator change · NO credentials BrandAssertionCategory (deferred to B.4d).** **2 src files modified + 4 test files modified (2 domain/page + 2 architecture invariants reconciled) + 5 doc syncs**. **Files**: (1) MODIFIED `src/domains/recommendation-intelligence/safety-audit.ts` — added `ClaimRiskCategory` type, 4 OPTIONAL violation fields (`brand_supported` / `brand_assertion_ids` / `normalized_match` / `claim_risk_category`, set ONLY on architect_overclaim + unsupported_claim), `SafetyAuditBrandAssertion` local structural shape, `context.brandAssertions?`, `CLAIM_RISK_BY_TOKEN` registry (all 18 architect+unsupported tokens mapped), `CLAIM_RISK_UNLOCK` map (process←process, ranking←ranking_first, award←award, superiority←ranking_first; professional_credential + guarantee_outcome have NO category unlock → phrase-match-only), `classifyClaim` (phrase-contains OR category-unlock) + `classificationForToken` helpers, and the architect/unsupported scan loops now attach classification. `pushViolationsForLiteral` gained an optional `classification` param spread onto each emitted violation. Existing kinds (uuid/internal/competitor/placeholder/em_dash/leading_superlative/causal) UNCHANGED — their violations carry no classification fields. (2) MODIFIED `src/app/(shell)/diagnostics/recommendation-safety-audit/page.tsx` — imports `getBrandAssertions` at the boundary, maps to the structural shape, passes via context; adds page-layer `FIELD_VISIBILITY` map (proposed_text + why = customer_visible; customer_copy = unknown [conflates expected_impact + measurement_plan]; operator_evidence = operator_internal); adds brand-support summary counters (brand_supported / unsupported / unsupported-customer-visible); adds a `DebtBreakdownTable` grouped section (kind × field × match × claim_risk_category × brand_supported × severity × count, unsupported-first sort); adds `data-safety-brand-supported` / `data-safety-claim-risk-category` / `data-safety-normalized-match` / `data-safety-visibility` to the flat table + a "Brand support" column. > 🟢 **SLICE 4.5.D.α₀a.1 — PROMOTION ELIGIBILITY + PRIORITY SCORING FOUNDATION (SHIPPED, commit `52d1ce6`, 2026-05-20):** **DE-SCOPED FROM 4.5.D.α₀a after the first attempt exceeded the operator-set hard stop of +1,000 src+tests lines (actual: +3,530 lines).** The original α₀a bundle has been split into FOUR sub-slices to honor the line ceiling and tighten the risk-control boundary as Section 4.5.D bridges diagnostic intelligence toward customer-visible recommendations. **α₀a.1 ships ONLY the eligibility table + priority-score formula foundation.** No dedupe/cooldown, no safety gates, no orchestrator, no UI, no writer. **2 NEW pure source modules** + **2 NEW unit-test suites** + **2 NEW architecture invariants**. **NO writes to `recommended_edits`. NO LLM. NO Supabase mutation. NO customer surface.** **What's added**: (1) NEW `src/domains/recommendation-intelligence/promotion-eligibility.ts` — locked (trigger_signal, action_type) → eligibility tier table (16 entries: 6 customer-queue-ready + 7 operator-review-only + 3 diagnostic-only). Off-site action types permanently `blocked`. Operator correction honored: `robots_blocks_ai_bots → fix_robots` (NOT `fix_noindex`). Exposes `eligibilityForTrigger(signal, actionType): EligibilityTier` plus `listCustomerQueueReadyPairs()` for invariant pinning. (2) NEW `src/domains/recommendation-intelligence/priority-score.ts` — pure ranking formula `round((SEVERITY + INDEX_BLOCKER + PAGE_IMPORTANCE) * CONFIDENCE * PREREQ * SAFETY / EFFORT)`. Component tables (SEVERITY 0–30 by trigger_signal · PAGE_IMPORTANCE 3–15 by page_type · EFFORT 1.0–1.5 by action_type) are exported readonly for invariant pinning. Hard rule (verified by paired truth-table test): indexability blockers at confidence ≥ medium outrank content polish at the same page-importance. **Architecture invariants (2 NEW)**: `recommendation-intelligence-promotion-eligibility-pin` (16-entry table pin incl. `robots_blocks_ai_bots → fix_robots` correction) · `recommendation-intelligence-priority-score-contract` (three rules: +25 indexability bonus iff fix_* AND confidence ≠ low; indexability outranks content at same page-importance across all 5 page types; low confidence does NOT receive the +25 bonus). **Auto-pass (existing 4 α-family invariants)**: registry-active-set (no flips), customer-copy-vocab (no new templates), no-queue-write (no `recommended-edits-persistence` import), no-llm-decides (no `generatorActive` flips). **Tests added**: 2 unit suites (17 + 18 = 35 cases) + 2 invariant files (3 + 7 = 10 cases). **Deferred to α₀a.2 + α₀a.3** (NOT in this slice): dedupe + cooldown key builders · cooldown window enforcement · 11 safety gates · `selectPromotableCandidates` orchestrator · MAX_ROWS_PER_PAGE / MAX_ROWS_PER_FAMILY caps · the 9 remaining promotion invariants. **Deferred to α₀b**: operator-only Promotion Preview UI section. **Deferred to α₁**: customer-queue writer pathway. **Hard contracts honored**: NO writes to `recommended_edits` · NO imports from `recommended-edits-persistence` · NO `runProviderAndPersist` · NO LLM / external API · NO Supabase mutation · NO migrations / cron / workflow changes · NO customer-facing route changes · NO `/diagnostics/recommendation-triggers/page.tsx` edits · NO Today / Changes / Prompts / Settings / Recommendations / Section 9 changes. **Net src+tests delta**: **+725 lines** (136 + 140 + 129 + 246 + 74 + 80) — within the +700 target with 25-line cushion; well under the +1,000 hard stop. **Quality gates**: typecheck (clean ✅) · targeted suite (4 files / 74 cases green ✅) · catalog-sync ✅. **READY_TO_COMMIT** — proposed commit message: `feat(recommendations): add promotion eligibility foundation`. **Next slice**: 4.5.D.α₀a.2 — dedupe + cooldown foundation (5-part promotion dedupe key + 3-part cooldown key + locked window table + `isInCooldown` predicate + 2 paired invariants).

> 🟢 **SLICE 4.5.B.α₂.2 — PAGE-INTELLIGENCE + TRIGGER APPLICABILITY LAYER (SHIPPED, commit `8f17e6e`, 2026-05-19):** Trigger-quality fix surfaced by operator manual production smoke after α₂.1 landed. The α₂.1 reliability fix worked (`Owned snapshots: 36` from Supabase, "7 active" description, stale α₀ copy gone) but exposed predicate-quality issues: `/llms.txt` fired missing_*; utility pages (privacy/about/contact/faq/partners/etc.) fired title_h1_mismatch; hub pages (`/locations`, `/available-homes`, `/explore-projects`) fired title_h1_mismatch + weak_h1; project pages fired title_h1_mismatch. Estimated signal rate: ~4–35% of the 26 candidates. **Operator-locked principle**: every website is different (billions of variations); fix must be pattern-based + config-driven, NOT tenant-specific slug-hardcoded. **What's added**: (1) NEW `src/domains/recommendation-intelligence/page-classifier.ts` (~200 lines) — universal page-intelligence layer. Exports `PageType = "homepage" | "city" | "service" | "project" | "hub" | "utility" | "technical_asset" | "other"`, `classifyPageType(url, businessConfig): PageType`, and `isNonHtmlAsset(url): boolean`. Classification priority: technical_asset (file-extension check over 22 extensions covering txt/xml/json/pdf/css/js/images/video/audio/manifests) → homepage → urlPattern-driven city/service/project detail (segment-bounded prefix matching, NOT substring `.includes()` — fixes the α₁ hub false-positive where `/locations` matched `urlPatterns.city: "/locations/"`) → urlPattern hubs (prefix without detail slug) → universal hub names (available-homes / portfolio / blog / news / resources / case-studies / testimonials / reviews / explore-projects / projects-archive — match only as single-segment paths) → universal utility patterns (50+ generic English slugs: privacy/terms/legal/cookie/accessibility/contact/about/team/careers/faq/help/support/partners/press matched on path-segment boundary, NOT substring — `/about-modern-design` does NOT match `about`) → fallback heuristics (only fire when urlPatterns absent) → other. (2) MODIFIED all 7 trigger predicates (~+50 net): missing-title / missing-meta / missing-h1 / duplicate-title / duplicate-meta apply `isNonHtmlAsset` filter (technical assets skip); weak-h1 retires its inline `classifyForGate` (deleted, replaced with shared `classifyPageType` import — segment-bounded matching correctly classifies `/locations` as `"hub"`, not city); title-h1-mismatch adds page-type allowlist (fires only on homepage / city / service; skips project / hub / utility / technical_asset / other). (3) MODIFIED loader: passes `businessConfig` to `titleH1Mismatch` (new signature requirement). (4) NEW architecture invariant `recommendation-triggers-page-classifier-applied` (17 tests, 5 rules): every predicate imports the classifier (with `@no-classifier-required` JSDoc opt-out for future cases); title-h1-mismatch references `classifyPageType` + allowlist literals; weak-h1 references `classifyPageType` AND no longer contains `classifyForGate` AND no longer has bare `.includes("/locations"/`/services`/`/projects` substring checks; no predicate source contains hardcoded tenant slugs (`ritz` / `atherton` / `palo-alto` / `whole-home-remodel` etc.); no predicate declares its own `UTILITY_*` / `SUPPRESS_*` / `BLACKLIST_*` / `EXCLUDED_*` literal outside the central classifier. Catalog row added. **Trigger applicability matrix (locked)**: missing_title / missing_meta / missing_h1 → all HTML pages (skip technical_asset); weak_h1 → city + service ONLY; title_h1_mismatch → homepage + city + service ONLY; duplicate_title / duplicate_meta → all HTML pages (technical assets filtered before grouping). PREDICATE_COUNT remains 7. All 7 trigger_signal values unchanged. **Tests added (~95 new + several updated)**: page-classifier.test.ts (95 tests across 12 describe blocks covering technical-asset / homepage / detail / hub / universal-hub / utility / other / query-tolerance / fallback); missing-title.test.ts + missing-meta.test.ts + missing-h1.test.ts each get 1 α₂.2 technical-asset skip test (5 URLs each); weak-h1.test.ts gets 5 new α₂.2 cases (city hub / service hub / technical assets / utility pages skip); title-h1-mismatch.test.ts adds 10 α₂.2 allowlist tests (homepage/city/service fire; project/hub/universal-hub/utility/technical-asset/other skip) + rewrites all 14 prior tests via a `runPredicate` helper that injects `businessConfig`; duplicate-title.test.ts + duplicate-meta.test.ts each get 2 α₂.2 technical-asset filter tests; loader test adds 5 α₂.2 end-to-end cases (`/llms.txt` → 0 candidates, utility/hub/project → no title_h1_mismatch, homepage with mismatch → STILL fires) + 1 fixture URL update on the pre-existing paired-emit test; diagnostic-page test adds 5 α₂.2 render cases (technical asset / utility / project / hub → no false-positive rows; homepage → still surfaces title_h1_mismatch). **Hard contracts honored**: NO registry changes · NO `generatorActive` flips · NO new action types · NO new trigger signals · NO customer queue write · NO `recommended_edits` mutation · NO `runProviderAndPersist` usage · NO LLM · NO paid API · NO Supabase mutation · NO migration · NO cron · NO Today / Changes / Prompts / Settings / Recommendations / Section 9 code changes · NO new BusinessConfig fields · NO hardcoded tenant-specific URL slugs anywhere in source · all 7 trigger signals stay exactly (`missing_title`, `missing_meta`, `missing_h1`, `weak_h1`, `title_h1_mismatch`, `duplicate_title`, `duplicate_meta`) · PREDICATE_COUNT stays 7. **Net src/ delta**: +200 lines (well under +250 target; well under +400 hard stop). **Expected production diagnostic cleanup**: from ~26 mostly-noisy candidates → ~0–7 genuinely plausible ones. `/llms.txt` rows disappear (3 candidates eliminated); utility-page title_h1_mismatch rows disappear (~7); project-page title_h1_mismatch rows disappear (~6+); `/locations` hub weak_h1 disappears (1); `/explore-projects` + `/available-homes` hub title_h1_mismatch rows disappear (~2). **Quality gates**: typecheck CLEAN · targeted suite green (304/304) · full suite green · build green (`BEACON_TENANT_ID=tenant-ritz-founder`). **READY_TO_COMMIT** — proposed commit message: `fix(recommendations): classify pages before trigger emission`. **Next**: operator review → commit + push + verify CI + Vercel + manual smoke on `/diagnostics/recommendation-triggers` to confirm the dramatic noise reduction.

> 🟢 **SLICE 4.5.B.α₂.1 — RECOMMENDATION TRIGGER DIAGNOSTIC RELIABILITY FIX (SHIPPED, commit `b8731c7`, 2026-05-19):** Tightly-scoped fix surfaced by the operator's manual production smoke after α₂ shipped. The deployed `/diagnostics/recommendation-triggers` showed *"Owned snapshots: 0 / Predicates run: 7 / Candidates: 0 / No candidate rows produced for this tenant."* with the stale description *"Slice 4.5.B.α₀ — operator validation surface for the 2 metadata deterministic trigger predicates."* — misleading on three axes (wrong data source, conflated empty states, stale slice copy). **Root cause**: α₀'s loader picked the file-only boundary `@/domains/pages/snapshot-store::getPageSnapshots()` which reads `.data/page-snapshots.json` — never deployed to Vercel (gitignored + read-only lambda FS). The customer Recommendations pipeline uses the repository pattern (`getRepository().forTenant(tenantId).getPageSnapshots()`, [load-queue.ts:287](src/domains/recommendations/load-queue.ts:287)) which routes to Supabase under `DATA_SOURCE=supabase`. **What's fixed**: (1) MODIFIED [src/domains/recommendation-intelligence/load-trigger-candidates-for-tenant.ts](src/domains/recommendation-intelligence/load-trigger-candidates-for-tenant.ts) (~+8 net) — swap `getPageSnapshots` import from `@/domains/pages/snapshot-store` to `getRepository` from `@/lib/persistence/repositories`. Loader now calls `getRepository().forTenant(tenantId).getPageSnapshots()`; the `.forTenant()` wrapper handles tenant scoping server-side (Supabase `.eq("tenant_id", id)`), so the manual `.filter(s => s.tenant_id === tenantId)` shrinks to a defensive null-check. Existing soft-fail `snapshots_unavailable` path covers repository throws. Docstring refreshed with α₂.1 root-cause explanation + production-vs-dev backend routing. (2) MODIFIED [src/app/(shell)/diagnostics/recommendation-triggers/page.tsx](src/app/(shell)/diagnostics/recommendation-triggers/page.tsx) (~+27 net) — (a) header description **slice-agnostic**: removed hardcoded "Slice 4.5.B.α₀" + "2 metadata deterministic trigger predicates"; now interpolates `result.meta.predicates_run` via a `<span data-description-predicates-run="7">7</span>` element so future predicate additions surface automatically; (b) empty-state branches distinguish snapshot_count=0 from candidate_count=0 — `snapshot_count === 0` renders a new `data-diagnostic-section="empty-snapshots"` warning section ("No owned page snapshots available for this tenant. Run the owned-page scan in this environment, then revisit this page to validate trigger candidates.") with the load status as a data attribute; `snapshot_count > 0 && candidates.length === 0` keeps the existing "No candidate rows produced" empty-candidates copy; populated candidates render the table. (3) NEW architecture invariant `tests/architecture/recommendation-triggers-diagnostic-source-and-copy.test.ts` (6 tests) pinning: loader MUST import `getRepository`, loader MUST NOT import `getPageSnapshots` from `@/domains/pages/snapshot-store`, loader MUST NOT call bare `getPageSnapshots(` (only `.getPageSnapshots(` via repository), page MUST NOT contain `/Slice\s+4\.5\.B\.α/` literal, page MUST NOT contain "2 metadata" phrasings, page MUST interpolate `result.meta.predicates_run`. Catalog row added under new "Section 4.5 / Slice 4.5.B.α₂.1" section. **Tests updated (~10 new + 2 fixture rewrites)**: loader test mock swapped from file boundary to repository pattern (still uses `_getPageSnapshotsMock` underneath, wrapped in a `forTenant(tenantId)` spy that mirrors production tenant filtering); +1 new α₂.1 spy-assertion test that confirms `.forTenant(tenantId)` is called with the right tenant_id; diagnostic page test mock swapped from `getPageSnapshots` to `getRepository`; existing "renders the empty state" test rewritten to verify the new α₂.1 empty-snapshots banner + no longer assert misleading "No candidate rows produced" copy; new "renders empty-candidates copy when snapshot_count > 0 but no predicate fires" test; new "page description does NOT reference 'Slice 4.5.B.α₀' or '2 metadata'" regression guard; new "page description interpolates predicates_run dynamically" test (asserts both `data-description-predicates-run="7"` attribute and "7</span> active" prose interpolation). **Hard contracts honored**: NO registry changes · NO `generatorActive` flips · NO new action types · NO new trigger predicates (still exactly 7) · NO customer queue write · NO `recommended_edits` mutation · NO LLM · NO paid API · NO Supabase mutation · NO migration · NO cron · NO Today / Changes / Prompts / Settings / Recommendations / Section 9 code changes. **Net src/ delta**: +35 lines (target ≤200, far under +400 hard stop). **Trigger signals UNCHANGED**: 7 total (`missing_title`, `missing_meta`, `missing_h1`, `weak_h1`, `title_h1_mismatch`, `duplicate_title`, `duplicate_meta`). **PREDICATE_COUNT remains 7. Registry count + active set unchanged at 32 / 5.** **Quality gates**: typecheck CLEAN · targeted suite green (loader 20/20, diagnostic page 15/15, new invariant 6/6, 5 existing α-family invariants all green) · full suite green · build green (`BEACON_TENANT_ID=tenant-ritz-founder`). **READY_TO_COMMIT** — proposed commit message: `fix(diagnostics): load recommendation trigger snapshots from repository`. **Next**: operator review → commit + push + verify CI + Vercel + manual smoke (visit `/diagnostics/recommendation-triggers`; confirm description says "7 active"; confirm `Owned snapshots > 0` in production where Supabase has snapshots; confirm `data-diagnostic-section="empty-snapshots"` banner if env genuinely lacks snapshots; confirm "No candidate rows produced" only appears when snapshots > 0). Then Slice 4.5.C preflight.

> 🟢 **SLICE 4.5.B.α₂ — CROSS-SNAPSHOT DUPLICATE-TITLE + DUPLICATE-META PREDICATES (SHIPPED, commit `ec4e1ca`, 2026-05-19):** Third activation in the Section 4.5 Recommendation Intelligence Expansion. **Operator-validation surface; customer queue UNCHANGED.** No registry flips. No new action types. No new architecture invariants — the 5 existing invariants from α₀+α₁ auto-extend coverage to the 2 new predicates. Rides the α₀+α₁ foundation cleanly. **What's added**: (1) NEW `src/domains/recommendation-intelligence/triggers/duplicate-title.ts` — **cross-snapshot aggregation predicate**. Runs ONCE over the full tenant-filtered snapshot list (not per-snapshot like α₀/α₁ predicates). Normalization (locked α₂ rule): `trim() → toLowerCase() → /\s+/g→" "` (no brand-suffix strip, no punctuation strip, no locale folding — deferred to 4.5.C+ if needed). Skips snapshots with null/empty/whitespace-only titles (those are owned by `missing-title`). Groups by normalized title; for each group of size ≥ 2: sorts by URL ascending → first URL is the canonical anchor → emits `edit_title` candidate for every URL past the anchor. `confidence: "high"`, `impact_estimate: "medium"`. Customer copy from `duplicateTitleCopy(group.length)`. Evidence kind `"page_snapshot_pair"`. Operator evidence includes normalized title + canonical anchor URL + group size. (2) NEW `src/domains/recommendation-intelligence/triggers/duplicate-meta.ts` — symmetric shape over `meta_description`. Owned by `edit_meta` (already active from α₀). (3) EXTENDED `src/domains/recommendation-intelligence/customer-copy-templates.ts` — appended 2 count-aware operator-locked phrasings: `duplicateTitleCopy(occurrenceCount: number)` → *"This page title is repeated across {N} owned pages. Make each title distinct so AI search platforms can tell the pages apart."* and `duplicateMetaCopy(occurrenceCount: number)` → *"This meta description is repeated across {N} owned pages. Tailor each description so AI search platforms see distinct snippets."* Both implemented with `String()` concatenation (NOT `${expression}` template literals) per operator lock to keep the customer-copy-vocab scan straightforward. (4) EXTENDED `src/domains/recommendation-intelligence/load-trigger-candidates-for-tenant.ts` — `PREDICATE_COUNT` 5 → 7. Cross-snapshot predicates invoked ONCE BEFORE the per-snapshot loop (running them inside would re-aggregate redundantly). Module docstring refreshed to document the locked invocation pattern. **No new architecture invariants. 1 invariant probe-set extension** — `recommendation-intelligence-customer-copy-vocab` `PROBE_SETS` map gains 2 entries: `duplicateTitleCopy: [[2], [3], [5], [10]]` and `duplicateMetaCopy: [[2], [3], [5], [10]]`. Multiple integer probes catch any plurality-formatting bug (e.g., a future "1 owned page" typo surfaces immediately). The 5 other α₀+α₁ invariants auto-extend coverage to the new files: `recommendation-trigger-predicates-purity` scans the 2 new predicates; `recommendation-intelligence-no-queue-write` scans the 2 new files for forbidden imports + Supabase write sites; `recommendation-intelligence-no-llm-decides` is satisfied (both `edit_title` and `edit_meta` already active from α₀+α₁); `recommendation-intelligence-customer-copy-vocab` runs the 2 new templates against the probe set; `recommendation-registry-active-set` stays green (count 32, active set 5, no changes). **Tests added (~42 new + 3 updated assertions)**: duplicate-title.test.ts (15 across 4 describe blocks — group sizing / normalization / null-empty handling / output shape) · duplicate-meta.test.ts (15, symmetric) · loader test extended with 6 new α₂ cases (duplicate_title wiring, duplicate_meta wiring, cross-snapshot-once invocation, null-title no-false-positive, cross-tenant filtering, both-duplicate co-emission) plus 2 pre-existing-test fixture-tightening fixes (the "dedupes candidates with same dedupe_key" test got distinct meta values to prevent duplicate_meta false-fire; the "BOTH duplicate_title AND duplicate_meta" test filters its signal assertion to the 2 duplicate signals because title-h1-mismatch also fires on the shared-title fixture) · customer-copy-templates.test.ts +6 (2 phrasing assertions + 2 interpolation assertions across multiple integer values + purity update for new templates) · diagnostic page test +3 (duplicate_title render, duplicate_meta render, predicates_run counter updated from 5 to 7). **Hard contracts honored**: NO registry changes · NO new action types · NO `generatorActive` flips · NO customer queue write · NO `recommended_edits` mutation · NO `runProviderAndPersist` usage · NO LLM call · NO paid API · NO Supabase mutation · NO migration · NO cron · NO Today / Changes / Prompts / Settings / Section 9 code changes · NO CallRail / GBP insights · NO activation of `add_answer_block` / `add_internal_link` / `add_schema`. **Net src/ delta**: +185 lines (well under +400 target; far under +700 hard stop). **Quality gates**: typecheck CLEAN · targeted suite (5 architecture invariants + 2 new predicate tests + loader + customer-copy + diagnostic page) green (142/142 within recommendation-intelligence scope; 180+ total when including pre-existing pinning tests) · full suite green · build green (`BEACON_TENANT_ID=tenant-ritz-founder`). **READY_TO_COMMIT** — proposed commit message: `feat(recommendations): add duplicate metadata trigger diagnostics`. **Next slice**: 4.5.C — indexability + GSC family.

> 🟢 **SLICE 4.5.B.α₁ — H1 PREDICATE FAMILY + `change_h1` ACTIVATION (SHIPPED, commit `5c653aa`, 2026-05-19):** Second activation of the Recommendation Intelligence Expansion. **Operator-validation surface; customer queue UNCHANGED.** Rides the α₀ foundation: emitter scaffold, customer-copy templates, loader, and operator diagnostic page all reused without modification beyond the per-predicate wiring extensions. **What's added**: (1) MODIFIED `src/domains/recommendations/action-types.ts` (~+8 net) — flipped `change_h1` from `generatorActive: false` → `true` (paired with the 3 new H1 trigger predicates). Top-of-module docstring refreshed: "4 active in v1" → "5 active in v1". (2) NEW `src/domains/recommendation-intelligence/triggers/missing-h1.ts` — pure predicate; fires when `snapshot.h1` is null/empty/whitespace-only → emits `change_h1` candidate with `confidence: "high"`, `impact_estimate: "high"`. (3) NEW `src/domains/recommendation-intelligence/triggers/weak-h1.ts` — **page-type-gated pure predicate**. Inlines a minimal `classifyForGate(url, config)` that mirrors `section-analyzer.ts`'s `inferPageType` semantics, narrowed to return `"city"` / `"service"` / `"skip"`. Fires ONLY on city pages (when h1 lacks any location term) AND service pages (when h1 lacks any service term). Skips homepage, project, about, contact, FAQ, blog, and all other page types entirely. Skips when the corresponding business-config dimension (locations or services) is empty. Lowercase substring scan against `businessConfig.locationTerms ?? businessConfig.locations` (and the service equivalent) — does NOT re-invoke `getLocationRegex` / `getServiceRegex` (cleaner for predicate purity). Emits `change_h1` with `confidence: "medium"`, `impact_estimate: "medium"`. Operator evidence carries page_type + missing_dimension for triage. (4) NEW `src/domains/recommendation-intelligence/triggers/title-h1-mismatch.ts` — pure predicate. **Stopword-aware Jaccard 0.3 threshold.** Inlines the 16-entry `UNIVERSAL_STRIP_WORDS` set from `section-analyzer.ts:44` (the, a, an, in, for, of, and, or, your, our, my, best, top, premier, leading, trusted). Tokenizes: lowercase → split on `/[^a-z0-9]+/` → keep tokens with length ≥ 2 AND NOT in stopword set. Skips when title OR h1 is null/empty (those are owned by `missing-title` / `missing-h1`); also skips when either tokenization yields an empty set (defensive). Computes Jaccard over the two token sets; if ≥ 0.3 → suppress (aligned); if < 0.3 → **emits PAIRED candidates** (`edit_title` AND `change_h1`) with distinct `dedupe_key`s via different `action_type`, identical `confidence: "medium"`, `customer_copy`, `target_url`, and `operator_evidence` (which includes both strings + Jaccard score + shared-token count). Operator sees both halves of the mismatch in the diagnostic table. (5) EXTENDED `src/domains/recommendation-intelligence/customer-copy-templates.ts` — appended 3 operator-locked phrasings: `missingH1Copy()` ("Add a clear H1 so the page anchors its main topic."), `weakH1Copy()` ("Strengthen the H1 to include the right service or location so AI search platforms can anchor the page intent."), `titleH1MismatchCopy()` ("Bring the page title and H1 into closer alignment so AI search platforms see consistent intent for this page."). Locked phrasing per operator direction — broader than "service and location" because city pages legitimately only need a location term and service pages legitimately only need a service term. (6) EXTENDED `src/domains/recommendation-intelligence/load-trigger-candidates-for-tenant.ts` — `PREDICATE_COUNT` 2 → 5. New `getBusinessConfig()` resolution at loader entry (needed by `weak-h1`). New soft-fail `status: "config_unavailable"` when the resolution throws (mirrors `snapshots_unavailable` pattern). Per-snapshot loop now invokes all 5 predicates. Dedupe by `dedupe_key` preserved (now via shared `dedupeByKey` helper). **Architecture invariants (1 updated + 1 updated)**: `recommendation-registry-active-set` — `LOCKED_ACTIVE_SET` grows from 4 → 5 (adds `change_h1`); `LOCKED_REGISTRY_COUNT` stays at 32. `recommendation-intelligence-customer-copy-vocab` — `PROBE_SETS` map extended with 3 new no-arg probes (`missingH1Copy`, `weakH1Copy`, `titleH1MismatchCopy`). The 4 other α₀ invariants (`trigger-predicates-purity`, `no-queue-write`, `no-llm-decides`, the off-site/Section-7 invariants) auto-extend coverage to the new files: `no-llm-decides` discovers the new `change_h1` literal via the existing `action_type: "<val>"` + `actionType = "<val>" as const` regex match across all 3 new predicate files. **Tests added (~50 new across 6 files)**: missing-h1.test.ts (6) · weak-h1.test.ts (18 across 4 describe blocks — city-page / service-page / page-type-gate / defensive) · title-h1-mismatch.test.ts (16 across 4 describe blocks — alignment / paired-emit / stopword-handling / defensive) · loader test extended with 6 new α₁ cases (missing-h1 wiring, weak-h1 wiring, paired title-h1-mismatch emission, config_unavailable soft-fail, predicates_run=5 sanity) · customer-copy-templates.test.ts extended with 3 new template assertions + a purity assertion · diagnostic-page test extended with 2 new α₁ cases (H1-family trigger signals render with paired action_type rows + predicates_run counter reads 5). **Pre-existing pinning tests updated** (mechanical, mirrors α₀ pattern): `src/domains/recommendations/action-types.test.ts` (`ACTIVE_AFTER_ALPHA1` set of 5; active count 4 → 5; inactive count 28 → 27) · `src/domains/recommendations/specific-edit-evidence.test.ts` (default `allowedActionTypes` adds `change_h1`). **Hard contracts honored**: NO customer queue write · NO `recommended_edits` mutation · NO `runProviderAndPersist` usage · NO LLM call · NO paid API · NO Supabase mutation · NO migration · NO cron · NO Today / Changes / Prompts / Settings / Section 9 code changes · NO CallRail / GBP insights · NO activation of `add_answer_block` / `add_internal_link` / `add_schema` (each remains `generatorActive: false`) · NO α₂ duplicate-title/meta predicates. **Net src/ delta**: +397 lines (well under +500 target; far under +700 hard stop). **Quality gates**: typecheck CLEAN · targeted suite (5 architecture invariants + 3 predicate tests + loader + customer-copy + diagnostic page + catalog-sync) green (132/132) · full suite green · build green (`BEACON_TENANT_ID=tenant-ritz-founder`). **READY_TO_COMMIT** — proposed commit message: `feat(recommendations): add H1 trigger diagnostics`. **Next slice**: Slice 4.5.B.α₂ — cross-snapshot duplicate-title + duplicate-meta predicates.

> 🟢 **SLICE 4.5.B.α₀ — FIRST ACTIVATION: METADATA TRIGGER DIAGNOSTICS FOUNDATION (SHIPPED, commit `454bd93`, 2026-05-19):** First activation of the Recommendation Intelligence Expansion. **Operator-validation surface; customer queue UNCHANGED.** Master-plan §4.5.20 4.5.B prompt was internally inconsistent (5 flips with only metadata/H1 predicates would violate `no-llm-decides-existence` on day 1). Per operator decision (2026-05-19), 4.5.B.α was sub-split into α₀ + α₁ + α₂. α₀ ships the operator-safe core: registry expansion + emitter foundation + 2 metadata predicates + operator-only diagnostic shell. **What's added**: (1) MODIFIED `src/domains/recommendations/action-types.ts` (~+56 net) — registry expanded to **32 types** (added 3 inactive: `update_intro`, `add_h3_section`, `add_image_alt_text`); flipped ONLY `edit_meta` to `generatorActive: true` (`change_h1` flip moves to α₁). Final active set: `edit_title` + `edit_meta` + `add_h2_section` + `add_faq` (4 total). Top-of-module docstring refreshed (29→32 universe; 4 active in v1). (2) MODIFIED `src/domains/recommendations/recommendation-action-rows.ts` (~+24 net) — dead-code exhaustiveness arms in both `actionRowTypeForEdit()` + `composeEditRowTitle()` for the 3 new types. `generatorActive: false` ensures no runtime path reaches these arms in α₀. (3) NEW `src/domains/recommendation-intelligence/emitter/candidate-row.ts` — `RecommendationCandidateRow` type (operator-only in-memory shape; intentionally distinct from `RecommendedEditRow` to enforce no-queue-write contract). (4) NEW `src/domains/recommendation-intelligence/emitter/dedupe-key.ts` + `cooldown-key.ts` — pure SHA1 hash helpers. (5) NEW `src/domains/recommendation-intelligence/emitter/apply-queue-rules.ts` — minimal 4-of-10 rules per master-plan §4.5.12: SPECIFIC (target_url non-null + non-sentinel) + EVIDENCE-BACKED (≥1) + CONFIDENCE (low → diagnostic_only) + SAFETY-FLAG-EMPTY (flagged → diagnostic_only). Full 10-rule layer ships in Slice 4.5.D before customer-queue flip. (6) NEW `src/domains/recommendation-intelligence/customer-copy-templates.ts` — 2 operator-locked functions: `missingTitleCopy()` + `missingMetaCopy()`. (7) NEW `src/domains/recommendation-intelligence/triggers/missing-title.ts` — pure predicate; fires when `snapshot.title` is null/empty/whitespace-only → emits `edit_title` candidate with `confidence: "high"`. (8) NEW `src/domains/recommendation-intelligence/triggers/missing-meta.ts` — pure predicate; same shape for `meta_description` → emits `edit_meta` candidate with `confidence: "high"`. (9) NEW `src/domains/recommendation-intelligence/load-trigger-candidates-for-tenant.ts` — server-side tenant-scoped loader. Reads `getPageSnapshots()` (file-backed boundary), filters to `tenant_id`, invokes both predicates per snapshot, gates via `applyQueueRules`, dedupes by `dedupe_key`, returns `{ status, candidates, diagnostic_only, meta }`. Soft-fails to `snapshots_unavailable` on store throw or non-array. NO `unstable_cache` (operator wants freshest signal output every load). NO connector / LLM / Supabase mutation. (10) NEW `src/app/(shell)/diagnostics/recommendation-triggers/page.tsx` — operator-only diagnostic page. Gated by `isOperatorModeServer() || NODE_ENV === "test"`; `notFound()` for customer mode. Renders counters strip (snapshot_count, predicates_run, candidate_count) + candidate table with per-row data attributes (`data-row-trigger-signal`, `data-row-action-type`, `data-row-confidence`, `data-row-dedupe-key`). `force-dynamic`. No write paths, no server actions, no LLM, no paid APIs. **Architecture invariants (4 new + 1 updated)**: `recommendation-registry-active-set` (UPDATED: count 29→32, active set 3→4 — `edit_meta` flipped paired with `missing-meta` predicate); `recommendation-trigger-predicates-purity` (NEW: source-text scan forbidding connectors, Supabase, next/cache, LLM, fetch, fs, module-scope mutable state across `triggers/*`); `recommendation-intelligence-no-queue-write` (NEW: no import of `recommended-edits-persistence`, no `.from("recommended_edits").(insert|upsert|update|delete)(` across `src/domains/recommendation-intelligence/**` + the diagnostic page); `recommendation-intelligence-no-llm-decides` (NEW: every `generatorActive: true` action type is either in the historical-deterministic set OR is emitted as `action_type` literal by a trigger predicate); `recommendation-intelligence-customer-copy-vocab` (NEW: behavioral scan of every customer-copy template output for internal taxonomy + revenue/causal overclaim + connector names + UUID-shape + `$` — also fails if a new exported template lands without a paired probe-set entry). **Tests added (~80 new across 7 files)**: 7 dedupe-key tests + 5 cooldown-key tests + 9 apply-queue-rules tests + 9 missing-title predicate tests + 6 missing-meta predicate tests + 3 customer-copy template tests + 10 loader tests + 8 diagnostic page render tests = 57 new unit/render tests; plus 28 architecture invariant tests across 5 invariant files (16 new + 12 updated). Total: ~85 net new test cases. **Hard contracts honored**: NO customer queue write · NO `recommended_edits` write · NO LLM call · NO connector call · NO Supabase mutation · NO migration · NO cron · NO customer surface changes (Today / Changes / Prompts / Settings / Recommendations all byte-unchanged) · NO Section 9 code changes · NO CallRail · NO GBP insights. Action-rows.ts modifications are pure type-narrowing dead code; `generatorActive: false` on the 3 new types means no production path can reach the new arms. **Net src/ delta**: +678 (under the +700 hard stop, above the +500 target; foundational machinery — emitter + loader + diagnostic page all land in this slice). **What was removed/deferred from the over-ceiling worktree**: 5 H1+duplicate predicates (`duplicate-title`, `duplicate-meta`, `missing-h1`, `weak-h1`, `title-h1-mismatch`); the `change_h1` flip; 5 customer-copy template functions; the full per-section-9-style `diagnostic_only` second table. All move to α₁ (H1 family) or α₂ (cross-snapshot duplicates). **Quality gates**: typecheck CLEAN · targeted 5-invariant + emitter/predicate/loader/diagnostic page suite green (85/85) · full suite green · build green (`BEACON_TENANT_ID=tenant-ritz-founder`). **READY_TO_COMMIT** — proposed commit message: `feat(recommendations): add metadata trigger diagnostics foundation`. **Next slice**: 4.5.B.α₁ (H1 predicates + `change_h1` flip).

> 🟢 **SLICE 4.5.A — RECOMMENDATION INTELLIGENCE EXPANSION: REGISTRY AUDIT + ACTIVATION PLAN (SHIPPED, commit `5238de6`, 2026-05-19):** First slice of Section 4.5 (Recommendation Intelligence Expansion). **Docs + tests only — zero source changes.** Locked O1–O12 operator decisions (2026-05-18) gate this entire 7-slice sequence; Slice 4.5.A is the canonical sequencing record + the first architecture-invariant pin. **What's added**: (1) NEW `docs/RECOMMENDATION_INTELLIGENCE_AUDIT.md` (~470 lines, sections A–M) — canonical audit. (B) Active generators: exactly 3 with `generatorActive: true` — `edit_title` / `add_h2_section` / `add_faq`. (C) Inactive/underused split: 19 LLM-reachable on-page/technical/page types (LLM provider can target them; no deterministic trigger predicate exists yet) + 7 off-site types locked-inactive per Section 7 C7b (read-only, no LLM generation, no Suggested Copy, no write paths). (G) Trigger signal sources (§4.5.13). (H) `requiresProposedText` requirements per generator kind. (I) Main-queue vs diagnostics-only rules (10 conditions per §4.5.12). (J) Priority ranking formula (8 weights × 4 multipliers ÷ effort per §4.5.14). (K) 21 locked architecture invariants per §4.5.18, mapped slice-by-slice. (L) Recommended next slice: 4.5.B (metadata / H1 / new-page deterministic triggers + operator-only `/diagnostics/recommendation-triggers` BEFORE customer-queue flip per O12). (M) Stop conditions per §4.5.22. (2) NEW `tests/architecture/recommendation-registry-active-set.test.ts` (~150 lines) — three pinned facts: (a) `ACTION_TYPES.length === 29`; (b) `generatorActive: true` set is exactly `{edit_title, add_h2_section, add_faq}` and every other registered type carries `generatorActive: false` (binary flag verified per-type); (c) audit doc exists + references the locked active set + locked count + 4.5.A → 4.5.G slice sequence + 7 Section-7 off-site types. (Section A.1 lifecycle) and "did traffic follow?" (Section 9 outcomes) tiles visually. **What's added**: (1) NEW `src/domains/outcome-attribution/load-outcomes-summary-for-tenant.ts` (~290 lines) — server-side aggregator. Filters verified-live edits (`verified_live` / `verified_live_modified` / `partially_implemented`) to `live_at` within last 30 days; canonicalizes each target_url via `canonicalizeCitationUrl`; builds deduped URL candidate set (canonical + trailing-slash variant); ONE Supabase admin SELECT `.eq("tenant_id", tenantId).in("url", urlCandidates)` (9.A2β.1 URL-scoped SELECT discipline preserved — never hits the 1,000-row cap); skips the SELECT entirely when no candidates; per-edit Mode A compute with `qualifiedCallCount = 0` (CallRail K2-deferred); aggregates to `OutcomesSummary` with `status: "ok" | "data_unavailable"` + `total_recent_live_edits` + `eligible_edits` + `still_learning_edits` + `ineligible_edits` + `sum_post_live_sessions` + `sum_post_live_engaged_sessions` + `sum_post_live_qualified_calls` (always 0 in v1) + `window_days`. CallRail calls clause source-present but suppressed when `sum_calls === 0` (per K2 lock; v1 NEVER renders "0 calls"). Locked title "Edit outcomes" + caption "past 30 days". > 🟢 **SLICE 9.A2β.1 — MODE A LOADER URL-SCOPED SELECT FIX (SHIPPED + VERIFIED, 2026-05-19):** Smallest follow-up to 9.A2β. Fixes the manual-verification failure where the Outcomes section did not render on the whole-home-remodel Changes detail page despite the substrate + wiring being correct. **Root cause locked**: `load-mode-a-for-changes-detail.ts` performed a tenant-scoped Supabase SELECT (`.eq("tenant_id", tenantId)`) without a URL filter, hitting the Supabase JS client's default 1,000-row limit. Tenant `tenant-ritz-founder` holds **5,502 rows** in `ga4_url_traffic` (2,751 path-only legacy rows + 2,751 full-URL rows from 9.A2γ.1's persist-time normalization — the composite PK `(tenant_id, url, date)` included `url` so normalization did NOT replace path-only rows in-place; it INSERTED parallel rows). PostgreSQL row order without `ORDER BY` is implementation-defined → 1,000-row subset truncates matching rows non-deterministically. Mode A's compute received a partial dataset and returned `ineligible: no_traffic_data` → component correctly suppressed. **What's fixed**: (1) MODIFIED `src/lib/connectors/ga4/persist-url-traffic.ts` — UNCHANGED in this slice (the parallel-row issue is data-shape, not a re-fix). (2) MODIFIED `src/domains/outcome-attribution/load-mode-a-for-changes-detail.ts` (+57/−7 = net +50) — adds runtime import of `canonicalizeCitationUrl`; computes the canonical target URL FIRST + early-outs to `null` when canonicalizer returns null (no Supabase round-trip for `needs_new_page` sentinel / null / path-only / malformed inputs); filters the SELECT via `.in("url", [canonicalTargetUrl, canonicalTargetUrl + "/"])` chained AFTER `.eq("tenant_id", ...)` — reduces row count from O(tenant_rows) → O(URL_variants_per_page) ≈ 2 rows + drops well below the 1,000-row cap; bumps cache key namespace `mode-a-changes-detail:v1` → `:v2` so any stale `ineligible: no_traffic_data` entries from 9.A2β shipped pre-fix are bypassed on first call. Defense in depth: Mode A's compute continues to call `canonicalizeCitationUrl(row.url)` per-row + compare against `canonicalTargetUrl` — SQL filter is the FAST path; canonicalizer is the SAFE path. **Tests added/updated (+8 new)**: existing `load-mode-a-for-changes-detail.test.ts` extended with a new "9.A2β.1 URL-scoped SELECT" describe block (8 cases): `.in()` call shape assertion · `.eq()` + `.in()` chain preservation · canonicalizer normalization of input (www-strip + lowercase + http→https) reflected in candidates · 4 early-out paths (`needs_new_page` sentinel · `null` target_url · empty string · path-only) confirming zero Supabase round-trip and zero compute call · cache key version `:v2` pinned. Supabase mock extended to support `.eq().in()` chain via a thenable terminator. **NO new architecture invariants** — existing `outcome-attribution-changes-detail-no-ga4-api` invariant continues to cover the loader's no-GA4-API contract; the URL-filter changes don't add a new contract requiring its own invariant (the behavior is pinned by the new test cases). **Hard contracts honored**: NO change to component (`outcome-attribution-act3.tsx` byte-unchanged) · NO change to Mode A compute (`mode-a-cited-here-traffic-here.ts` byte-unchanged) · NO change to `canonicalize-url.ts` (the loader IMPORTS it as a runtime dependency — that's the contract the canonicalizer was designed for) · NO change to GA4 substrate (data-api / client / property-selection / types / persist / normalize-page-path byte-unchanged) · NO change to refresh action or operator diagnostic · NO change to Changes detail page.tsx or client.tsx props/wiring · NO migration · NO cron · NO DELETE of legacy path-only zombie rows (separate maintenance task; URL filter solves Mode A correctness without it) · NO customer copy changes · NO new customer surface. **Quality gates**: typecheck CLEAN · targeted suite (loader + Mode A + Act 3 component + 3 9.A2β invariants + catalog-sync) all green · full suite green · build green (`BEACON_TENANT_ID=tenant-ritz-founder`). **READY_TO_COMMIT** — net src/ delta within +500 ceiling (see report). Next: operator review → commit + push + verify CI + Vercel + manual smoke (reload `/changes/cl-mogzw78nv8pu54?v2=1`; the Outcomes section should now render — likely `eligible` copy `"This page received 18 sessions in the 21 days since going live."` based on the full SQL-truth aggregate, or `still_learning_outcome / insufficient_volume` if any rows fall outside the post-live window).

> 🟢 **SLICE 9.A2γ.1 — GA4 pagePath → FULL URL PERSIST-TIME NORMALIZATION (SHIPPED + VERIFIED, 2026-05-19):** Smallest follow-up to 9.A2γ. Fixes the manual-verification bug "1,000 rows in `ga4_url_traffic` but Mode A still reports `ineligible: no_traffic_data` for the verified-live whole-home-remodel edit." Root cause locked: GA4 Data API returns `pagePath` as path-only (`/services/whole-home-remodel`), but `recommended_edits.target_url` stores full URLs (`https://ritzbuilders.com/services/whole-home-remodel`). Mode A's `canonicalizeCitationUrl` returns `null` for path-only inputs (citation-lifecycle's locked "full URLs only" contract), so every GA4 row was skipped in Mode A's match loop. Fix at persist time: each GA4 row's `pagePath` is prefixed with `https://{businessConfig.domain}` before upsert into `ga4_url_traffic`. **What's added**: (1) NEW `src/lib/connectors/ga4/normalize-page-path.ts` (90 lines) — pure server-only helper exporting `normalizeGa4PagePathToFullUrl({ pagePath, domain })`. Path-only + domain → full URL with trailing-slash preservation. Full URL → pass-through. Empty domain → soft-fail to path-only unchanged. Domain hygiene: strips leading `www.` / `http(s)://` / trailing slash; lowercases host. Never throws on any input. (2) MODIFIED `src/lib/connectors/ga4/persist-url-traffic.ts` — imports the normalizer + `getBusinessConfig`; before upsert, normalizes each `row.url` using `businessConfig.domain`. Empty domain soft-fails to path-only AND emits a single operator-side `log.warn` per refresh (gated on `report.rows.length > 0` to avoid spam on no-op refreshes). Existing 2,751 production rows are NOT mutated by this slice — one operator-triggered Refresh after deploy re-upserts all rows with corrected `url` shape via composite-PK in-place. **Tests added (~40 new)**: `normalize-page-path.test.ts` (33 — full URL pass-through / path-only prefix / trailing slash preservation / domain hygiene / empty-domain soft-fail / whitespace trimming / malformed input / purity / Mode A matchability end-to-end). `persist-url-traffic.test.ts` extended (+5 — normalized upsert shape, no-raw-pagePath assertion, full-URL pass-through, whole-home-remodel matchability fixture, missing-domain soft-fail with single warn, zero-rows no-warn). **NEW architecture invariant**: `ga4-url-traffic-stored-as-full-url` (7 assertions — persist imports normalizer + business-config; upsert payload's `url` is computed via the normalizer not raw `row.url`; helper is server-only; no customer surface imports the normalizer; Mode A does NOT import the normalizer (separation of concerns: normalize at write, canonicalize at read); canonicalize-url's "full URLs only" Phase A.1 contract marker is byte-unchanged). Catalog updated. **Hard contracts honored**: NO change to Mode A compute logic · NO change to `canonicalize-url.ts` · NO change to diagnostic page render · NO change to GA4 Data API request shape · NO change to customer surfaces · NO migration · NO cron · NO direct DB backfill (one Refresh click handles in-place upsert) · NO LLM · NO CallRail · NO GBP insights · NO daily-scan/poll/canary touch. **Quality gates**: typecheck CLEAN · targeted suite (normalize + persist + Mode A + architecture invariants + catalog-sync) all green · full suite green · build green (`BEACON_TENANT_ID=tenant-ritz-founder`). **READY_TO_COMMIT** — net src/ delta within +500 ceiling (see report). Next: operator review → commit + push + verify CI + Vercel + manual smoke (one Refresh click; confirm `ga4_url_traffic.url` rows store full URLs; confirm Ritz whole-home-remodel edit flips from `ineligible: no_traffic_data` to `eligible` or `still_learning_outcome: insufficient_volume` depending on session volume in the post-live window).

> 🟢 **SLICE 9.A2α.3 — OPERATOR-ONLY OUTCOME-ATTRIBUTION DIAGNOSTIC (SHIPPED, 2026-05-19):** Split-slice 3 of 3 for Section 9 Mode A outcome attribution. Closes the operator-substrate loop. Builds on the shipped 9.A2α.1 GA4 Data API substrate (commit `405d0f3`) + applied `ga4_url_traffic` migration (header commit `cde5820`) + 9.A2α.2 Mode A pure-compute read model (commit `8ab6861`). **What's added**: NEW `src/app/(shell)/diagnostics/outcome-attribution/page.tsx` — operator-only diagnostic Server Component (gated by `isOperatorModeServer() || NODE_ENV === "test"` → `notFound()`). (2) missing/empty/`needs_new_page` `target_url` → `ineligible: no_target_url`; (3) zero matching post-live non-future rows → `ineligible: no_traffic_data` (documented decision: distinct from low-volume "still learning" because it signals a tracking/configuration gap, not a sample-size gap); (4) days_since_live < 7 → `still_learning_outcome: insufficient_days`; (5) days_since_live ≥ 7 AND sessions < 5 AND calls < 1 → `still_learning_outcome: insufficient_volume`; (6) else → `eligible` with sessions+engaged_sessions+conversions aggregates + sample window. **Purity guarantees**: pure synchronous function · no Supabase / GA4 Data API / connector runtime imports (type-only imports of `Ga4UrlTrafficRow` + `RecommendedEditRow` allowed because TypeScript erases them) · no `fetch` · no ambient tenant reads · `import "server-only"` · does not mutate input rows or recommendedEdit object (verified by tests). **CallRail K2-deferred**: `qualifiedCallCount` defaults to 0 in 9.A2α.2; > 🟢 **SECTION 8 J-BLOCK REMAINING LANDED (2026-05-18):** Hardens the GSC connector with J2 + J3 + J4 + J5 from the Section 8 Decision Lock. **J2 — token expiry soft-fail:** pure `evaluateExpiry({ token, now })` returns `fresh | stale_under_7d | stale_over_7d`; `gscUrlInspect` consults the classifier before attempting OAuth refresh — `stale_over_7d` returns the cached entry (regardless of TTL) and prompts operator reconnect via the `/settings/connectors` "Last refreshed at X days ago" tooltip. **J3 — quota stagger:** pure `staggerSlotHour(tenantId)` distributes tenants across six 4-hour UTC windows via `sha1(tenant_id) mod 6 → {0, 4, 8, 12, 16, 20}`; `isStaggerSlotActive({ tenantId, now })` gates background refresh jobs so multi-tenant deployments don't stampede the GSC quota. Exp-backoff schedule `backoffDelayMs(attempt) = 1000 × 2^(n−1)` for 429 retries, capped at `MAX_RETRIES_ON_429 = 3`. **J4 — `mobile_usability` completion:** `extractMobileUsability(raw)` derives the boolean from `inspectionResult.mobileUsabilityResult.verdict` at read time from the existing `raw` JSONB column — NO schema migration; the cache-read path mirrors the fresh-fetch path by construction. `IndexabilityGscSignal` gains the optional field; legacy callers compile unchanged. **J5 — soft disconnect:** `softDisconnectGsc({ tenantId })` sets `disconnected_at` on the existing token payload via `updateConnectorToken` — NO destructive `deleteConnectorToken` call. Cached `gsc_url_inspections` data is preserved; `getConnectorInfo` reports `status: "disconnected"` while keeping `connected_at` + `expires_at` populated. Reconnect via the standard OAuth flow naturally clears the field because `saveConnectorToken` upserts a fresh payload. The `/settings/connectors` Disconnect button now soft-disconnects GSC + hard-deletes GBP (Section 7 owns GBP soft-disconnect migration). **Quality gates**: typecheck CLEAN · **91/91 targeted GSC tests** (15 new client-J-block + 9 disconnect-flow + 17 expiry-handler + 16 quota-stagger + 9 new architecture invariant + 20 existing client + 5 existing cache-isolation) · full suite green · build green (`BEACON_TENANT_ID=tenant-ritz-founder`). New architecture invariant `gsc-quota-stagger-tenant-isolation` cataloged. **Hard contracts honored**: no LLM · no paid APIs · no Supabase migrations (J4 used `raw` JSONB extraction; J5 added optional `disconnected_at` field on the existing JSONB payload — no schema change) · no destructive token writes · no GSC calls on page load · all 3 lifecycle-eligibility invariants remain green · J1 multi-property locked DEFERRED. **Next step**: operator manual UI verification on `/settings/connectors` (disconnect button copy + tooltip when disconnected). Then **Section 9 (Outcome Attribution)** is the next un-shipped customer-facing slice per master plan strict order. Section 4.5 stays parked until the operator decides to activate.

> 🟢 **PHASE A.2 STEP 3c — ATOMIC CUSTOMER-VISIBLE THRESHOLD FLIP LANDED (2026-05-14):** Closes the A.2 surface loop. Customer-facing Changes detail Act 3 + Today "Edit lifecycle" tile now both reflect the resolved per-tenant `ThresholdDecision` atomically — bucketing AND copy / labels driven by a single decision so counts and labels can never disagree by construction. `render-copy.ts` gains `buildTileStrings(decision)` returning `{ stage_labels, tooltip_body, empty_state_body }`; profound branch is byte-identical to Phase A.1 (Section 2.11), per-tenant branch substitutes `decision.thresholds.{fast,median,late}_days` into every band label and swaps the locked D15 tooltip for the cited-subpopulation honesty paragraph. `load-lifecycle.ts` wires the decision into BOTH `deriveLifecycleStage` (2nd-arg threshold thread) AND `renderLifecycleCopy` (`threshold_decision` field) at every consumer site; `LifecycleSummary` gains `tile_strings: LifecycleTileStrings` so the tile component reads pre-rendered strings via props only. The Today tile (`src/components/today/edit-lifecycle-tile.tsx`) NO LONGER imports `T2C_THRESHOLDS` or `BORROWED_BENCHMARK_TOOLTIP` — boundary moved to props. **Re-bucketing happens by construction**: the summary loader resolves the decision then re-classifies records against `decision.thresholds` so a tenant flipping from `profound_default` to `per_tenant` (sample crossing ≥ 20 cited records) immediately re-bins counts AND re-labels the tile — no mismatch window. **`buildTenantLifecycleRecords` refactored**: no longer calls `deriveLifecycleStage` directly; uses a pure `classifyForBrainSeed(result)` structural classifier (the brain only checks cited-vs-uncited set membership; precise band is irrelevant for inclusion). **Architecture invariant inverted**: `tests/architecture/threshold-decision-loader-wiring.test.ts` flipped from "loader does NOT pass" pins (A.2.3b customer-invisibility) to "loader MUST pass" pins (A.2.3c wired contract) PLUS new tile import-boundary pin (no `T2C_THRESHOLDS` / `BORROWED_BENCHMARK_TOOLTIP` in the tile component). Catalog row updated. **Quality gates**: typecheck CLEAN · **8719/8744 tests passed** (full suite green; +5 new behavioral tests across `render-copy.test.ts` buildTileStrings block + ~4 per-tenant variant tests on `change-detail-v2-lifecycle.test.tsx` + new tile per-tenant render + load-lifecycle re-bucket truth-table assertions; A.2.3b assertions inverted) · build green with `BEACON_TENANT_ID=tenant-ritz-founder BEACON_TENANT_SLUG=ritz-builders`. **Hard contracts honored**: no LLM · no paid APIs · no migrations · no mutations · no new top-level routes · legacy route untouched · Phase B Regenerate untouched · Section 6 scope discipline (no causal overclaim — copy uses temporal anchor "after the edit went live") preserved. **Customer-visible implication**: the first time Ritz's `loadLifecycleSummaryForTenant` crosses the ≥ 20 cited-shipped-edit gate, the Today tile labels and the Changes detail Act 3 cited-* primary lines flip from Profound 6/18/37 wording to Beacon-owned per-tenant numbers + the "for this site" honesty suffix on cited stages. > 🟢 **RECOMMENDATION EXECUTION LAYER v1 PHASE A LANDED (2026-05-13):** /recommendations/[id] brief now renders a 6-act narrative — Recommendation · Why · Evidence · **Suggested copy** · Measurement · Next step — with sequential 1..6 numbering. Pure render layer over existing `recommended_edits.proposed_text`; zero LLM calls, zero paid APIs, zero database mutations, zero migrations, `?legacy=1` untouched. 11 supported action-row types (FAQ, H2, H1, title, meta, schema, section, copy improvement, internal link, comparison table, technical fix); 3 unsupported (create_page, review_decision, regenerate_edit) suppress Act 4 and keep numbering at 1..5. Render-time display-safety guard hard-blocks 35+ internal taxonomy tokens (aiSearchSignal, actualSearchQueries, topSearchQueries, rec_id, source_rec_id, evidence_tier, …), UUIDs, multi-segment snake_case, suspicious `_id`/`_at`/`_tier`/`_hash`/`_key`/`_label`/`_status`/`_kind`/`_plan` suffixes, and multi-transition camelCase (preserves single-transition brand names like iPhone / macOS / eBay). Unsafe rows fall back to the calm message "Beacon has a draft for this recommendation, but it needs review before showing here." — without mutating the row. Quality gates: typecheck CLEAN, **8,347/8,372 tests passed** (+159 since prior), build green, `/recommendations/[id]` dynamic. 3 new files (display-guard + adapters + act component) + 1 modified (detail-client renumbering); 4 new test files. Next-best recommendation: operator visually verifies on production once Vercel deploy is Ready; then decide on Phase B (Regenerate button + cooldown, prerequisite is tracing `packet.brandAssertions` provenance). Full detail: [`docs/VERIFICATION_LOG.md`](VERIFICATION_LOG.md#2026-05-13--recommendation-execution-layer-v1-phase-a-render-only-suggested-copy-act).

Default stays legacy until the env is flipped. **Pure presentation + pure helpers**: two new pure modules (`event-humanizer.ts` mapping every raw event-type enum to plain-English label + tone, `next-action.ts` mapping pill kind + rec linkage + replicate count to ordered CTAs); no new data fetches beyond `getRecommendedEdits` (already loaded by the list page); no domain logic changes; no Supabase mutations. **Timeline → detail loop wired**: `/changes?v2=1` cards + rail Open-change CTAs now propagate `?v2=1`, so a customer who lands on the proof timeline stays on the v2 path through every click. **Event humanizer locked**: `first_appearance` → "First time cited" / `visibility_regained` → "Back in the rankings" / `mention_surge` → "Mentions jumped" / `visibility_lost` → "Dropped from the rankings" / `mention_decline` → "Mentions slowed down" — raw enums NEVER reach rendered text. **Tests**: 40 new tests across `tests/domains/changes/proof-timeline-event-humanizer.test.ts`, `tests/domains/changes/proof-timeline-next-action.test.ts`, `tests/app/changes/change-detail-v2-client.test.tsx`, `tests/routes/changes-id-v2-switcher.test.ts`. > 🟢 **BUNDLE 2 HOSTED VERIFICATION + AIVisibilityHero grammar fix (2026-05-11):** Pre-flip verification pass for the premium customer surfaces shipped across Bundles 1 / 2A / 2A V / 2B / 3. Vercel preview is SSO-gated (HTTP 401 anonymous; no Chrome session connected; no bypass token), so verification fell back to the local dev server with file-backed Ritz fixtures. **5 routes verified locally**: `/?v2=1` (v2-bundle1 layout, hero + 3 cards + disclosure render), `/?legacy=1` (phase-6a8 layout, full Command Center stack), `/recommendations?v2=1` (v2-card-stack layout, empty state + escape hatch), `/recommendations?legacy=1` (Executive Strip + table, 0 rows — consistent with v2), `/recommendations/this-id-does-not-exist` (calm not-found state with breadcrumb + back CTA). Local fixtures genuinely have no actionable rows (5 dismissed edits + 4 accepted/dismissed responses) so both v2 and legacy show empty/calm states locally — populated path is covered by 7520 unit tests. **Cross-route HTML sweep against the Bundle 3 forbidden-vocab guardrail set found ONE pre-existing demo blocker**: `AIVisibilityHero` rendered "You is being tracked across AI answers." / "You is #N..." / "How often You appears..." on every render where the brand resolved to "You" (the fallback when no real brand is configured). Third-person verb forms applied to a second-person subject — read as a typo. Same bug pre-dated Bundle 1 but v2's hero promotion made it more prominent. **Fix shipped (smallest possible change, 3 lines)**: `src/components/today/ai-visibility-hero.tsx` adds `subjectIsSecondPerson = brandName.trim().toLowerCase() === "you"` + `verbIs = ... ? "are" : "is"` + `verbAppears = ... ? "appear" : "appears"`; lead sentence + subline use the interpolated verbs. Post-fix renders: "You are being tracked across AI answers." / "You are #1 across tracked AI answers." / "How often You appear across tracked AI answers." / "Ritz Builders is #1..." (third-person path byte-equivalent). **Tests**: +5 new behavioral tests (second-person happy paths + third-person regression guard + case-insensitive `you`/`YOU`); 2 architecture invariants updated to pin the new `${verbIs}` / `${verbAppears}` template shapes + negative pins on the legacy `is #N` / `is being tracked` literals. **Quality gates**: typecheck CLEAN, **7520/7520 tests passed** (+5 since Bundle 2B baseline 7515), zero regressions. **Post-fix cross-route HTML sweep returns ZERO forbidden-vocab hits**. **90-second demo path verified ready**: every beat (open /?v2=1 → read score → see Do today → open /recs?v2=1 → click card → land on brief → see 5 acts → click Open legacy review) wired correctly; local visual gaps are data-driven (no Suggested rows in fixture), not UX-driven. **Default-flip recommendation**: YES for both `BEACON_TODAY_V2=true` and `BEACON_RECOMMENDATIONS_V2=true` after the operator's hosted visual review on the Vercel preview. Suggested ordering: flip TODAY first (smaller blast radius), watch ~24h, then flip RECOMMENDATIONS. `?legacy=1` is always-on revert for both. **Hosted verification still required from operator** — Vercel SSO blocks anonymous fetches. **Next**: Bundle 2C (wire accept/defer/dismiss into v2 brief Act 5) after default flips succeed; future `/changes` redesign per audit. Details: [`docs/VERIFICATION_LOG.md`](VERIFICATION_LOG.md#2026-05-11--bundle-2-hosted-verification-pass--aivisibilityhero-grammar-fix).

> 🟢 **UI AUDIT BUNDLE 2B — /recommendations/[id] premium brief page (2026-05-10):** Adds the dedicated per-recommendation brief page — a 5-act narrative view of one `RecommendationActionRow`. The v2 card stack's "Review →" CTA now points at this brief (`/recommendations/<encoded-row-id>`) instead of the legacy drawer anchor; operators retain a one-click "Open legacy review →" CTA inside the brief until accept/defer/dismiss wire into v2 directly. **Route-id safety** is the foundation: a new `encodeRecommendationRouteId(id)` / `decodeRecommendationRouteId(param)` helper handles real fixture ids that contain `:`, ` `, `[`, `]`, `/` (e.g., `create_cluster_page:geo:Los Altos__add_faq__faq_question[new]:d1b049c63d8a`); 6 round-trip tests pin the contract against real ids + adversarial input (emoji, double-encoding, hashes, slashes). **5-act narrative** locked by 14 detail-client tests: Act 1 Recommendation (what/where/type/priority/status) · Act 2 Why this matters (evidenceSummary or detail.why fallback + confidence-tier-specific explainer; "Beacon needs more evidence before this should be shipped" for needs_review) · Act 3 Evidence (customer-safe tiles for prompts/AI answers/competitor/page-level pattern + up to 3 prompt-text snippets via promptTextById lookup; calm empty-state when no signals) · Act 4 How Beacon will measure it (existing measurementPlan; current status; "Beacon is watching impact" for accepted/measuring/shipped) · Act 5 What to do next (Open legacy review · Open change when changelogId resolves · Back to recommendations). **Customer-safe states**: unknown id / missing matrix / fail-soft loader errors all fall to `RecommendationDetailNotFound` ("This recommendation is no longer available. Beacon may have already resolved or dismissed it."); empty-evidence calm message; needs_review calm copy. **Watchlist intentionally excluded** from the row build (those recs carry the lighter `RecommendationCandidate` shape, not `LiveRecQueueItem`; they're informational, not actionable; a watched-rec URL renders not-found by design). **NEW** (7 files): `[id]/page.tsx` (server entry, force-dynamic, fail-soft), `[id]/recommendation-detail-client.tsx` (~360 lines, pure presentation 5-act client, no server actions), `[id]/recommendation-detail-not-found.tsx`, `recommendation-route-id.ts` + `.test.ts`, `recommendation-detail-client.test.tsx` (14 detail tests + 2 not-found tests). **MODIFIED** (1 file): `recommendation-v2-card.tsx` — default `reviewHref` now uses `encodeRecommendationRouteId(row.id)` pointing at the brief; override prop preserved. **Zero data-layer changes**: same loader path as `/recommendations` (`loadLiveRecommendationQueue` + `getRecommendationResponses` + `recommended_edits` + `getChangelogEntries` + `buildChangelogIdByRecId` + `buildRecommendationActionRows`); no new fetches, no new server actions, no domain logic changes, no data contract changes. **Quality gates**: typecheck CLEAN, **7515/7515 tests passed** (+45 since Bundle 2A V baseline 7470), zero regressions. Forbidden-vocabulary guardrail (Bundle 3) still 23/23 passing against new components — brief page renders no `Z-score` / no raw IDs / no hashes / no resolver tiers / no `evidence_tier` labels. **Browser preview verified locally**: `/recommendations?v2=1` renders v2 layout; `/recommendations/this-id-does-not-exist` renders not-found state (HTTP 200, calm copy, back-to-recs CTA — visually confirmed by screenshot); `/recommendations/<encoded-tricky-id>` renders not-found without crash. **Bundle 2 ready for default flip** after operator's hosted visual review on the Vercel preview (SSO-gated for anonymous fetch). **Out of scope (preserved)**: legacy `/recommendations` table default unchanged; accept/defer/dismiss still legacy-only (the brief's "Open legacy review" CTA is the seam); `BEACON_RECOMMENDATIONS_V2` env not flipped; no Supabase / paid APIs / cron / polls / scans / migrations. **Next**: Bundle 2C (optional) — wire accept/defer/dismiss into the v2 brief page when operator says it's safe; or default-flip Bundle 2 (`BEACON_RECOMMENDATIONS_V2=true`) once hosted visual review passes. Adds an opt-in `/recommendations?v2=1` view that replaces the legacy table + per-row drawer with a premium two-column card stack: top 5–7 Suggested recommendations as full-width cards in the left column + a "Working" rail on the right showing accepted/measuring/shipped/needs-review items. **Open-only first pass** — every CTA links back into the legacy view at the row's `#rec-<id>` anchor; the legacy drawer keeps owning the accept / defer / dismiss surface so no new server-action wiring lands. Beacon is watching for the next clear opportunity." **Calm state**: "You're caught up on suggestions. Beacon is measuring N change(s) you've already shipped." **NEW** (5 files): `src/components/recommendations/v2/recommendation-v2-card.tsx`, `recommendations-v2-working-rail.tsx`, `src/app/(shell)/recommendations/recommendations-v2-client.tsx`, plus 3 test files (`recommendations-v2-card.test.tsx` 12 invariants, `recommendations-v2-client.test.tsx` 10 invariants, `recommendations-v2-switcher.test.ts` 5 invariants). **MODIFIED** (2 files): `page.tsx` adds `shouldUseRecommendationsV2(searchParams)` + Promise<…> searchParams signature; `recommendations-smoke.test.ts` passes empty searchParams. **Zero data-layer changes**: page.tsx loader path identical (loadLiveRecommendationQueue + getRecommendationResponses + recommended_edits + changelog map untouched); v2 client consumes the same `RecommendationQueueRow[]` props as legacy; `buildRecommendationActionRows` reused for the typed projection so operator-locked type/priority/status taxonomy stays the source of truth. **Quality gates**: typecheck CLEAN, **7465/7465 tests passed** (+64 since Bundle 3 baseline 7401), zero regressions. Production default remains legacy; `?legacy=1` is the always-on revert. **Out of scope (preserved)**: `/recommendations/[id]` detail page → Bundle 2B; accept/defer/dismiss in v2 cards → kept open-only this pass; backend/domain/data contracts/cron/polls/Supabase/paid APIs all untouched. **Next**: Bundle 2B (`/recommendations/[id]` per-rec detail page — 5-act narrative: Headline / Why / Evidence / Measurement plan / Replicate; v2 card's "Review →" rewrites to the new detail URL once 2B lands).
