> 🟢 **UI AUDIT BUNDLE 3 — forbidden customer vocabulary cleanup + guardrail (2026-05-10):** Third bundle of the maximum-depth UI/product audit (`~/.claude/plans/i-want-a-maximum-depth-curried-curry.md`). Strips operator/internal vocabulary out of customer-facing rendered copy across the 5 focus routes (`/today`, `/recommendations`, `/changes`, `/changes/[id]`, `/prompts`) plus shared components and adjacent surfaces (`/settings/prompts`, `/settings/methodology`, `/topics/opportunity/[id]`, replication briefs, prompt drilldown reasoning). Bundle 1 shipped a premium v2 /today layout — Bundle 3 stops customer copy from leaking the internal vocabulary that made the premium UI inherit an internal-tool feel. **Cleanups applied:** cron schedule leakage ("07:00, 08:30, 10:00 UTC" + "scheduled poll" → "daily AI check" / "checks AI visibility every morning"); statistical jargon ("Z-score" / "URL Z-score engine" → confidence labels and "Early per-page signal"; operatorDetail in `verdict-provenance.ts` keeps Z-score by design); "decision queue" loading/error → "Recommendations"; "evidence tier" rendered label → "confidence"; "native observations" / "native observation" → "AI readings" / "AI checks"; "pattern brain will sharpen" → "Beacon will sharpen"; raw event-type ENUMs in `/changes/[id]` (`First Appearance` / `Visibility Regained` / `Mention Surge` / `Visibility Lost` / `Mention Decline`) → plain English (`First time cited` / `Came back in the rankings` / `Mentions jumped` / `Dropped from the rankings` / `Mentions slowed down`); `Recommendation lifecycle status` aria-label and `Lifecycle` header → `Status`; "review poll health" / "Check poll health above" → "see data status"; "10:45 UTC" stale threshold → "the morning" plain-window; "next 10:00 UTC cron adds" (prompt drilldown) → "each daily check typically adds"; tooltip `title="Directional only — z-score above 1.2 but below the 2.0 strong-signal bar..."` on `EarlySignalPill` rewritten to plain English. **NEW architecture guardrail**: `tests/architecture/forbidden-customer-vocabulary-contract.test.ts` (~340 lines, 23 invariants) walks `src/app/(shell)/**` + `src/components/**` recursively, strips JSDoc + line + block comments AND `import` statements (so module-path identifiers like `@/lib/persistence/dual-write` don't false-positive), excludes `diagnostics/` and `*.test.{ts,tsx}` and `__tests__/`, and fails CI if any of 19 forbidden phrases reappear in customer-facing rendered copy. Per-rule `allowedFiles` lets specific code-identifier survivals pass (e.g., `verdict-provenance.ts` operatorDetail). **Reconciled 7 pre-existing tests** that pinned OLD strings: `today-empty-state-copy.test.ts`, `today-action-card-copy.test.ts` (T3 lineage bullet now pins `Signal strength: <X> confidence.` shape + new negative pin against `Z-score N.N`), `demo-path-fixes-2026-05-06.test.ts` (3 invariants flipped from cron-time positive pins to plain-English pins + cron-leak negative pins with `stripComments` for JSDoc tolerance), `data-freshness-heartbeat.test.tsx`, `prompts-teaser.test.tsx`, `opportunity-classify.test.ts`. **Files changed**: 28 total (12 customer-facing route files, 6 shared components, 4 domain reasoning-string modules, 6 reconciled tests; new guardrail). **Code identifiers preserved** (per audit "non-rendered code identifiers" exception): `lifecycleSummary` / `LifecycleTabClass` / `data-lifecycle-*` / `evidence_tier` field name / `stableKey` / `resolver_tier` / `engine_confidence` / `evidence_hash` / `first_appearance` enum KEY / `decision-matrix` module name / `@/domains/answer-intelligence/*` imports / `@/lib/persistence/dual-write` import. **Quality gates**: typecheck CLEAN, **7401/7401 tests passed** (+47 since Bundle 1 baseline 7354; 23 net-new guardrail invariants + Bundle 3 negative pins added across reconciled tests; zero regressions). Zero data-layer changes: no Supabase mutations, no paid APIs, no migrations, no domain logic, no schema, no data contracts, no cron / poll / scan / budget edits. Zero changes to v2 /today layout structure (Bundle 1 untouched; v2 cards inspected — already clean by construction). **Hosted impact**: both `/?v2=1` and `/?legacy=1` inherit cleaner copy automatically — the cleanup ships on this commit. **Next**: Bundle 2 (`/recommendations` table+drawer → card stack + per-rec `/recommendations/[id]` URL); the new card surfaces inherit clean copy, and the forbidden-vocabulary guardrail catches any regression. Details: [`docs/VERIFICATION_LOG.md`](VERIFICATION_LOG.md#2026-05-10--ui-audit-bundle-3-forbidden-customer-vocabulary-cleanup--guardrail) + plan file `~/.claude/plans/i-want-a-maximum-depth-curried-curry.md`.

> 🟢 **UI AUDIT BUNDLE 1 — gated v2 /today layout (2026-05-10):** Following the maximum-depth UI/product audit (`~/.claude/plans/i-want-a-maximum-depth-curried-curry.md`), this is the first of three staged bundles to take Beacon from "internal operator tool that shows 19 sections on /today" to "premium customer product with one obvious next action above the fold." Bundle 1 ships an opt-in v2 `/today` layout that compresses the above-the-fold view to one `AIVisibilityHero` (UX.6.3 component reused verbatim) + a 3-col grid of new cards: **Do today** (single primary action, no accordion, no duplication of v1's TopPick + PrimaryAction + DoNext stack), **Working** (live changes + pending implementation count, replaces v1 LifecycleStrip + ImplementationQueue + LiveChangesBlock triad), **Recent wins** (URL verdict proof + measured wins, capped at 3, replaces v1 MeasuredWins + Latest signal proof line). Below the fold, a native `<details>` "Show full data" disclosure exposes `VisibilityScoreChart` + `VisibilityLeaderboard` + `EnrichmentV2` + `PromptsTeaser` + full `ActionCard` wins + `ChangeReview` for operators who want depth. Operator-only sections (`DataFreshnessHeartbeat`, `PollHealthBlock`, `CommandCenter`, `TodayLifecycleStrip`, `TodayImplementationQueue`, `SinceLastVisit`, `TodayScanStrip`) are intentionally NOT rendered in v2 — reachable via the `/today?legacy=1` escape hatch link in the disclosure footer. **Switcher in `src/app/(shell)/page.tsx`**: `?legacy=1` → always v1, `?v2=1` → always v2, `BEACON_TODAY_V2=true` env → v2 default, otherwise v1 default (CURRENT PRODUCTION — env flag unset). `TodayPage` now reads `searchParams: Promise<...>` for the Next 16 / React 19 async-searchParams contract; both client variants receive identical `loadTodayPageData()` props + server actions. **Zero data-layer changes**: `today-data.ts` (2920 lines) untouched; `today-client.tsx` (legacy v1) untouched; all domain modules / repos / schemas / RLS / Supabase / cron / polls / scans / budget ledger / recommendation engine untouched; the 5-route navigation untouched. **NEW**: 4 files (`today-v2-client.tsx` + 3 v2 cards under `src/components/today/v2/`). **MODIFIED**: 2 files (`page.tsx` switcher, `today-smoke.test.ts` signature). **Quality gates**: typecheck CLEAN, **7354/7354 tests passed** (23 skipped, 384 files) — zero regressions. Browser preview verified locally with Ritz fixtures: `/?v2=1` renders `data-today-layout="v2-bundle1"` with real Do today / Working / Recent wins data; `/?legacy=1` renders unchanged `data-today-layout="phase-6a8"`; `/` (default) still renders v1. **Hosted rollout plan**: this commit pushes to `claude/infallible-neumann-407850` for Vercel preview → operator visual-confirms `/?v2=1` matches the audit's intent → operator decides whether to flip `BEACON_TODAY_V2=true` in Vercel for default-flip or keep at `?v2=1` opt-in for further iteration. `?legacy=1` is the always-on revert path — no code change needed if issues surface. **Next**: Bundle 2 (`/recommendations` from table+drawer to card stack + per-rec `/recommendations/[id]` URL), Bundle 3 (vocabulary cleanup across all routes — strip cron schedules, "Z-score engine", "evidence_tier", "lifecycle classification", "dual-write" from customer copy). Details: [`docs/VERIFICATION_LOG.md`](VERIFICATION_LOG.md#2026-05-10--ui-audit-bundle-1-gated-v2-today-layout-hero--3-cards) + plan file `~/.claude/plans/i-want-a-maximum-depth-curried-curry.md`.

> 🟢 **UX.6.3 — AI Visibility hero promotion on /today (2026-05-08):** Operator-approved hero promotion landed after this morning's twin cron fixes. Goal: make AI Visibility feel like the heart of Beacon, not just another chart section. **NEW `src/components/today/ai-visibility-hero.tsx`** — pure presentation component (no hooks, no fetch, no event handlers, server-renderable). Renders: executive header `AI Visibility` + dynamic `How often {brandName} appears across tracked AI answers.` copy; optional `Full sample` / `Partial sample` badge derived from cross-platform sparkline `sampleStatus`; lead sentence `{brandName} is #N across tracked AI answers.` (or first-reading fallback); 4-card metric strip with stable data-attrs (Visibility score `64.2%` + `+2.1 pts vs previous 14d` delta · Rank `#1 of 6 ranked` · Closest challenger name + `49.3% visibility` · Sample `12 days latest May 8`); per-platform footer `ChatGPT 42% primary · Perplexity 18% primary` + `Why this number?` link to `#today-metrics-disclosure`. **Wired in `today-client.tsx`** via `aiVisibilityHeroProps` `useMemo([visibilityData, visibilityWindow, enrichmentV2])` — pure projection over already-loaded inputs, no new I/O, no math change. Re-binds when chart's 7d/14d/30d/60d toggle changes. Closest challenger picks the competitor nearest to brand's rank (preferring "ahead" over "behind"). ChatGPT/Perplexity primary % from latest non-null `primaryRate` point on each `enrichmentV2.sparklines` entry. Sample state: `"full"` when both platforms hit `sampleStatus="enough"` else `"partial"` else `undefined` (don't show badge if can't honestly call it). **Leaderboard heading renamed**: `Visibility Score Rank` → `AI visibility leaderboard`; `Who gets mentioned most often in your topic` → `Who AI mentions most across tracked answers`. **AreaChart hover bug fixed**: pre-fix the tooltip wrapper was `{hoverIdx !== null && <div className="mt-1 ...">}` — entire row conditionally rendered, pushing downstream content (Compare competitors / Split by platform checkboxes) up/down on each hover. Post-fix the row is unconditional with `min-h-[14px]`; only content inside is conditional. Stable layout, no checkbox jump. Marker `data-area-chart-hover-row="true"` for invariants. Reconciled UX.5B.1 source-text marker (`visibility-hero-header` → `<AIVisibilityHero` component pin). NEW **22 behavioral tests** in `ai-visibility-hero.test.tsx` (happy path, all 4 metric data-attrs, empty/first-reading edge cases, partial-sample state, delta cases incl. honest null/limited-data, per-platform footer, no internal jargon, truncation). NEW **19 architecture invariants** in `tests/architecture/ux6-3-ai-visibility-hero-contract.test.ts` (file exists; pure presentation; data-attrs; lead sentence template; null-delta honest copy; no internal jargon; today-client wires correctly with useMemo deps + source order; section order CC → visibility-headline → DoNext → strip → impl → AQ → wins → metrics → ChangeReview preserved; memo body has no fetch/runners/mutations; leaderboard heading rename; AreaChart hover row uses `min-h-[14px]` + stable data-attr; old conditional-wrapper shape gone). **Quality gates**: typecheck CLEAN, **352/352 files / 6,565/6,565 tests** (+2 files, +62 tests vs morning baseline 350/6503), build green, all 3 integrity scripts PASS (Ritz UNCHANGED 9896/16521/28), watchdog YELLOW (queue-idle WARN only), LLM budget SHA `d36eed8c…` byte-identical. The page is friend-demo ready — pending operator visual verification on production. Visual check: hero at top of visibility section with all 4 metrics + per-platform footer + sample badge; chart + leaderboard sit below; leaderboard heading reads "AI visibility leaderboard"; hovering chart no longer shifts checkboxes; CC LatestReadingCard still owns poll-health badges (no fight with hero's metric metadata). > 🟢 **CRON GREEN — verify-persistence false-fail fix (2026-05-08, 2nd patch of the day):** Manual workflow_dispatch on `c9d0ae9` ran the cron successfully (compute-active-tenants ✅ poll-openai ✅ poll-perplexity ✅ rebuild-citation-evidence ✅) and **200 observations + 100 snapshots landed** for May 8. ~$3.07 native-poll cost recorded in `raw_poll_chunks.cost_usd`. The verify-persistence step then failed RED, but **only because the canary classifier was outdated — data is healthy**. **Failure class A (verifier outdated for the new direct-CLI workflow shape).** The Bundle 2 direct-CLI workflow (2026-05-07) polls 100 prompts in a SINGLE `observation_run` with scope_label `"Native perplexity poll · chunk offset=0 limit=all · 100/100 prompts · cost=$0.0998"`. The poll-health partitioner regex `/chunk offset=/i` over-matched both real chunks (limit=25) AND direct-CLI runs (limit=all), routing the latter into chunk-mode where `EXPECTED_CHUNKS=4` forced a "1/4 partial" verdict. **Fix**: tightened regex in `src/domains/observations/poll-health.ts` to `/chunk offset=\d+ limit=\d+/i` — `limit=all` now falls to the whole-mode branch which correctly reports 1/1 ok. Real chunked runs (limit=25) continue to flow through chunk-mode. Manual canary verification post-fix: `✓ perplexity ok 1/1 chunks, 100 prompts · ✓ chatgpt ok 1/1 chunks, 100 prompts · ✓ All platforms ok for 2026-05-08.` Tests: NEW 3 behavioral fixtures in `poll-health.test.ts` (direct-CLI shape ok, real chunks still ok, mixed-shape coexistence) + NEW `tests/architecture/poll-health-direct-cli-classifier-contract.test.ts` (3 invariants pinning the limit-arity-aware regex appears in BOTH filter calls, the over-broad form is gone from runtime code, source carries 2026-05-08 + direct-CLI + limit=all provenance trail). **Quality gates**: typecheck CLEAN, **350/350 files / 6,503/6,503 tests** (+1 file, +6 tests vs morning's `c9d0ae9` baseline 349/6497), build green, all 3 integrity scripts PASS (Ritz UNCHANGED 9896/16521/28), watchdog YELLOW (queue-idle WARN only), LLM budget SHA `d36eed8c…` byte-identical (recommendation-LLM ledger; native-poll cost is a separate per-chunk ledger). Zero OpenAI / paid polling triggered by this commit / production data mutations / new Supabase reads. **Status**: May 8 observations landed; /today is safe to mark fresh; **no need to rerun the cron**; the 2026-05-09 07:00 UTC scheduled cron will be the natural green-path test for the full UTC-day guard + direct-CLI classifier fix landing together. UX.6.3 hero promotion is now unblocked. Details: [`docs/VERIFICATION_LOG.md`](VERIFICATION_LOG.md#2026-05-08--verify-persistence-false-fail-fix-poll-health-partitioner-recognizes-direct-cli-shape).

> 🟡 **CRON FIX — UTC-day-anchored budget guard replaces rolling 20h (2026-05-08):** P0 cron-skip bug. The 2026-05-08 07:00 UTC scheduled poll silently skipped: upstream Poll Perplexity / Poll ChatGPT jobs marked status=success but printed `status=skipped_already_ran_today`, then verify-persistence failed RED with "Perplexity pending 0/4 chunks · ChatGPT pending 0/4 chunks". **Root cause**: `src/domains/observations/run-poll.ts` had a rolling 20-hour budget guard (`const BUDGET_GUARD_WINDOW_MS = 20 * 60 * 60 * 1000`). May-7 cron ran late (completed 16:11 UTC), May-8 07:00 UTC cron fired only 14h49m later — within the window — so `defaultHasRecentRun` returned true, `runNativePoll` returned `skipped_already_ran_today` BEFORE calling any paid API, `cron-poll.ts` exited 0 (skipped status not in the failure list), GH job marked success. The rolling-20h window was a silent ticking bomb that fired the moment any prior-day cron drifted past 11:00 UTC. **Zero paid API calls** on 2026-05-08; LLM budget SHA `d36eed8c…` confirmed byte-identical. **Fix**: replaced the rolling window with a UTC-day-anchored guard. NEW exported pure helper `utcDayStartIso(now?: Date): string` returns start-of-current-UTC-day ISO string. `defaultHasRecentRun(source, tenantId?)` query rewritten: `.gte("completed_at", utcDayStartIso())` + conditional `.eq("tenant_id", tenantId)`. Tenant scope NEW (Customer-2-multi-tenant safe). Skip-path note updated to "today (UTC)". Deps signature `hasRecentCompletedRun?: (source: string, tenantId?: string) => Promise<boolean>` — `tenantId` optional keeps existing single-arg test mocks compatible. File-top JSDoc carries provenance trail referencing this 2026-05-08 incident. **Behavior before vs after**: late-prior-day → today's scheduled poll: BLOCKED → RUNS. Same-UTC-day duplicate without --force: SKIPPED → SKIPPED (unchanged, correct). `force=true`: bypasses → bypasses (unchanged). Chunk-mode 15-min retry-dedupe: unchanged (separate concern). **Tests**: reconciled `run-poll.test.ts` ("last 20 hours" → "today (UTC)"; tenant-scoped `toHaveBeenCalledWith` assertion); NEW behavioral test "late prior-UTC-day run does NOT block today's scheduled poll" — explicit regression fixture for the May-7-16:11 → May-8-07:00 sequence; NEW `utcDayStartIso` truth-table (5 cases: mid-morning, last-millisecond, midnight roll, year-boundary, May-7/8 incident). NEW `tests/architecture/cron-utc-day-guard-contract.test.ts` (8 invariants: negative pins forbid `BUDGET_GUARD_WINDOW_MS` + "last 20 hours" + hours-shaped rolling cutoff; positive pins require `utcDayStartIso` exported + deps signature + call site passing both args + default-impl `.gte("completed_at", startOfDay)` + tenant-id conditional eq + skip-path note + JSDoc references 2026-05-08). **Quality gates**: typecheck CLEAN, **349/349 files / 6,497/6,497 tests** (+1 file, +16 tests vs UX.6.2 baseline 348/6481), build green, all 3 integrity scripts PASS (Ritz UNCHANGED 9896/16521/28), watchdog YELLOW (queue-idle WARN only), LLM budget SHA `d36eed8c…` byte-identical with UX.6.2. Zero OpenAI / paid polling / production data mutations / new Supabase reads / touches to onboarding / cron / RLS / auth / billing. **Safe to rerun May 8 cron via workflow_dispatch**: yes. Pre-flight `verify-daily-poll.ts` already confirmed READY. Operator clicks Actions → Daily native AI-visibility poll → Run workflow → Run. Expected total cost: **~$3.07** (~$0.10 Perplexity + $2.97 OpenAI). LLM budget SHA will increment after the rerun (normal post-paid-poll behavior). UX.6.3 stays halted until cron is green. Details: [`docs/VERIFICATION_LOG.md`](VERIFICATION_LOG.md#2026-05-08--cron-skip-bug-fix-utc-day-anchored-budget-guard-replaces-rolling-20h).

> 🟢 **UX.6.2 — Vocabulary + hierarchy cleanup on /today (2026-05-07):** Five fixes after UX.6.1 trust restoration. **Fix 1 — One vocabulary for the scan_findings layer.** Same underlying rows surfaced as three competing names ("133 important scan diffs" + "775 page issues" + "Scan diffs to review (7)") that read like three separate fires for what is actually three filter levels of the same array (7 ⊂ 133 ⊂ 775). NEW `src/lib/site-findings-labels.ts` is the single source of truth: exports `SITE_FINDING_NOUN`, `siteFindingNoun(count)`, `compactStripLabel({total, important, critical})`, `doNextHeadline({critical, important})`, `doNextSubtitle({total, critical, important, shownInPreview?})`, `recentSiteChangesHeading(count)`, `RECENT_SITE_CHANGES_SUBTITLE`. Three consumers updated: TodayDoNextCard (header "Site findings to review", helpers for headline + subtitle, CTA "Open site findings →" routes to /pages); TodayActionQueue strip (flows through compactStripLabel — "775 site findings · 133 important"; bonus: also fixes a prior literal `·` rendering bug); ChangeReview accordion (heading "Recent site changes (N)", customer-safe subtitle replacing "raw website differences found by Beacon's scanner"). **Fix 2 — Reduce scan-finding prominence.** DoNext review_site_findings branch keys tone off `criticalCount > 0`: critical → status-danger styling (kept); important-only → `border-border/60 bg-surface-inset/30 text-foreground` (NEW neutral muted, replacing the prior status-warning amber that made every important-priority count read as urgent). **Fix 3 — Recommendation duplication cleanup.** Pre-fix the same top-pick rec rendered three times: Command Center NextBestActionCard (line-clamp-3 multi-line rationale) + DoNext `decide_recommendation` branch (full reasoning) + Action Queue primary ActionCard (full body). Dropped DoNext's `decide_recommendation` discriminant + JSX branch entirely (CC owns the summary, AQ owns the workbench). New priority `pending_impl > review_site_findings > calm`. NextBestActionCard refactored to true SUMMARY shape: NEW `summarizeRationale(rationale)` extracts leading sentence (cuts on `". "` to avoid splitting on decimals; hard-caps at 110 chars with ellipsis); rendered in `line-clamp-1` instead of `line-clamp-3`; explicit "Full evidence in the action queue below" pointer at the bottom of the card; `data-next-best-action-reason="one-line"` + `data-next-best-action-cta="open-recommendation"` + `data-next-best-action-pointer="full-body-below"` for telemetry. **Fix 4 — Empty lifecycle compression.** TodayImplementationQueue empty branch now returns null (drops the `"Nothing waiting on you. Accepted edits live here..."` styled card that took ~3 lines for nothing). TodayLifecycleStrip computes `allActionableCountsZero` = pending + needs-review + not-found-after-7d all zero; when true, appends an inline muted `"· nothing waiting"` text after the chips and stamps `data-lifecycle-empty="true"` on the wrapper. Strip stays a single horizontal row. **Fix 5 — /today hierarchy preserved.** Source-order pin in `today-client.tsx`: CommandCenter → VisibilityScoreChart → TodayDoNextCard → TodayLifecycleStrip → TodayImplementationQueue → TodayActionQueue → wins → MetricsDisclosure → ChangeReview. Matches operator-required order: AI visibility → next best rec → wins → website findings (operations backlog). **NEW `tests/architecture/ux6-2-vocabulary-hierarchy-contract.test.ts`** (43 invariants across all 5 fixes + cross-cutting paid-API / mutation negative pins). **NEW `src/lib/site-findings-labels.test.ts`** (21 behavioral tests covering the helper truth tables — operator's exact "775 site findings · 133 important" + "Showing 7 most recent" examples pinned). UPDATED 4 prior test files (`today-do-next-card.test.tsx` drops decide_recommendation cases; `change-review.test.tsx` heading + subtitle pins to UX.6.2 vocabulary; `implementation-queue.test.tsx` empty-state flips from styled-card pin to "renders empty string"; `today-action-card-copy.test.ts` T2 strip pins migrate from "{N} page issue" to "flows through compactStripLabel"). Quality gates: typecheck CLEAN, **348/348 files / 6,481/6,481 tests** (+2 files, +67 tests vs UX.6.1 baseline 346/6414), build green, all 3 integrity scripts PASS (Ritz row counts UNCHANGED 9896/16521/28), watchdog YELLOW (queue idle WARN only), LLM budget SHA `d36eed8c…` byte-identical with UX.6.1. Zero OpenAI. Zero paid polling. Zero production data mutations. Zero new Supabase reads. Zero touches to onboarding / cron / RLS / auth / billing. **Result**: /today is materially less chaotic — one vocabulary across the page; calmer hierarchy (operations backlog reads as backlog, not as ER); clean summary/workbench split between Command Center and Action Queue; compressed empty lifecycle state. UX.6.3 (next pass) can take on AI Visibility hero promotion. Details: [`docs/VERIFICATION_LOG.md`](VERIFICATION_LOG.md#2026-05-07--ux62-vocabulary--hierarchy-cleanup-on-today-5-fixes).

> 🟢 **UX.6.1 — Trust restoration on /today (2026-05-07):** Three trust-breaking issues from the brutal /today touchpoint audit fixed in one pass. **Fix 1 — Brain readiness card lying in production.** The Command Center reads `.data/_reports/brain-health-*.json` but `.data/` is gitignored → never bundled to Vercel → card showed "Waiting for next reading" on every Vercel deploy, for every tenant, since UX.2 landed. NEW pure helper `deriveBrainSummaryFromCounts(input)` in `src/domains/today/command-center-data.ts` derives a grade-letter brain summary from already-loaded /today inputs (observations 7d count, recent snapshot count, owned URLs cited, recommendation queue size, platform coverage). Returns null when `totalObservationCount <= 0` so first-reading tenants still hit Gap F.1. Wired in `today-data.ts` via refactored `resolveCommandCenterFailSoft(args)` + sibling `deriveBrainFromTodayInputs(args)` that projects observations / snapshots / citationEvidenceIndex / recommendationQueueSize into the helper input. Zero new Supabase reads. **Fix 2 — Poll health false alarm before scheduled poll.** Operator opening /today between 00:00–08:00 UTC saw the alarming `PollHealthBlock` ("AI tracking has not run yet today / Perplexity pending / ChatGPT pending") for what is normal scheduling. NEW `src/components/today/poll-health-calm-banner.tsx` exports `<PollHealthCalmBanner />` (premium muted styling, "Next reading scheduled" copy, 07:00 UTC reference) + pure `isPreCronPending({ platforms, date, now? })` helper that returns true only when ALL platforms pending AND now < 08:00 UTC of the snapshot's date (07:00 UTC scheduled cron + 1h grace). `today-client.tsx` Tier 0 alerts now ternary `isPreCronPending(pollHealth) ? <PollHealthCalmBanner /> : <PollHealthBlock snapshot={pollHealth} />`. Partial / failed / post-cutoff still warn. **Fix 3 — Wins copy too caveated by default.** Old: "Citations rose after this change. URL-level signal detected; not proof of causation." (lead with disclaimer = sounds weak). New: positive direction → "This page gained citations after the change. Beacon is tracking the pattern so you can repeat what worked."; negative direction → "This page lost citations after the change. Beacon is tracking to see if it recovers." Methodology caveat ("URL-level correlation - this page's own citations moved - not proof of causation, and not a topic-wide trend.") preserved in `lineageBullets` so the "Why we suggest this" / detail drawer keeps the honest disclosure. **NEW `tests/architecture/ux6-1-trust-restoration-contract.test.ts`** (24 invariants across all 3 fixes) + 12 behavioral tests for `deriveBrainSummaryFromCounts` (first-reading null guard; Ritz-mature shape grades A across data/score/rec; soft-gap B/C/D; overall = worst-section) + 9 truth-table tests for `isPreCronPending` (empty, mixed status, pre-cutoff, exactly 08:00 cutoff, post-cutoff, malformed date, default `now`, single-platform). Reconciled 2 prior tests: `today-command-center-contract.test.ts` (resolver no-arg pin → args-shape pin; helper-body capture regex updated since `\n}` now matches close of args type) + `today-action-card-copy.test.ts` T3 (rationale pin updated to UX.6.1 positive/negative; Z-score/percent/caveat negative pins reanchored on the win-card-specific phrase since an unrelated pain-card rationale earlier in the file legitimately contains Z-score). Quality gates: typecheck CLEAN, **346/346 files / 6,414/6,414 tests** (+1 file, +57 tests vs EGRESS-P0 baseline 345/6357), build green, all 3 integrity scripts PASS (Ritz row counts UNCHANGED 9896/16521/28), watchdog YELLOW (queue idle WARN only), LLM budget SHA `d36eed8c…` byte-identical with EGRESS-P0. Zero OpenAI. Zero paid polling. Zero production data mutations. Zero new Supabase reads. **Result**: production /today now (1) shows a real grade-letter brain readiness card sourced from live-loaded data instead of "Waiting for next reading"; (2) a calm "Next reading scheduled" line between midnight and 8am UTC instead of an alarming poll-failure banner; (3) wins cards lead with the win + a forward-looking framing, methodology disclaimer one click away. The audit's three highest-trust-breaking issues are addressed. UX.6.2 (next pass) takes on F2 (scan-diff naming chaos), F4 (recommendation duplication 3×), F5+ items. Details: [`docs/VERIFICATION_LOG.md`](VERIFICATION_LOG.md#2026-05-07--ux61-trust-restoration-on-today-3-fixes).

> 🚨 **EGRESS-P0 — emergency Supabase egress mitigations (2026-05-07):** P0 incident triggered by operator screenshot showing **15.52 GB / 5 GB Supabase Free Plan egress** (3.1× over) + Vercel logs showing statement-timeout errors on `page_snapshots` (twice per /today render) + `prompt_answer_observations` (on /recommendations load-queue) + auth-loop noise from prefetch storming. **Recent UX work was NOT the cause** (Command Center reads disk JSON only; Executive Strip uses already-loaded rows). Root cause: existing route loaders had unbounded reads. **Five fixes shipped**: (1) `/recommendations` load-queue: `loadFreshCanonicalData()` now passes `{ observationsSince: 60d, snapshotsSince: 120d }`; (2) `/settings/prompts`: same with 1-day windows (page only consumes `trackedPrompts`); (3) `/prompts/[id]`: 60-day observation window; (4) tenant-scoped `getPageSnapshots` cap dropped 5000→500 + `select("*")` replaced with explicit column projection that omits 5-15KB-per-row heavy fields (`body_paragraph_sample`, `card_texts`, `internal_links`, `schema_entity_names`, `schema_validation_warnings`); (5) `/today` memoizes page_snapshots via new `getPageSnapshotsShared()` helper — 2 calls per render → 1. Plus 2 kill-switch env vars (`BEACON_COMMAND_CENTER_ENABLED=false`, `NEXT_PUBLIC_BEACON_RECOMMENDATIONS_STRIP_ENABLED=false`) for defense in depth. **Estimated egress reduction: 10–15× lower per page navigation** (~180-260 MB → ~12-22 MB per typical 4-route prefetch storm). 18 new architecture invariants pin every fix; 5 prior tests reconciled (relaxed `LIMIT 5000` pin to `≤ 5000`, widened pushdown look-ahead window for the multi-line projection, allowed kill-switch ternary, allowed any `loadFreshCanonicalData(` call form). Quality gates: 6354/6354 tests, build green, integrity scripts green (Ritz row counts UNCHANGED), LLM budget SHA byte-identical. **Production expectation post-deploy**: /today stops hitting page_snapshots statement-timeout, /recommendations stops hitting prompt_answer_observations statement-timeout, auth-loop subsides as prefetch storm becomes lighter, daily egress trajectory returns under the 5 GB Free Plan cap. **Operator decisions remaining**: (a) consider setting the kill-switch env vars in Vercel if production is still timing out (defense even though my investigation says new UX isn't the cause); (b) **Supabase Pro upgrade ($25/mo, 250 GB egress) recommended as durable fix** — Free Plan is genuinely tight for a system polling 100 prompts × 2 platforms daily, especially before customer-2 lands. Details: [`docs/VERIFICATION_LOG.md`](VERIFICATION_LOG.md#2026-05-07--egress-p0-emergency-supabase-egress-mitigations).

no paid APIs/fetch/openai/perplexity imports; source-order pinned (isProtectedSlug runs BEFORE loadAdminClient AND any .from("tenants") query; if (!confirm) check runs BEFORE any .delete() call); only runs main() when invoked directly (`require.main === module`) so test imports are safe. **NEW `tests/scripts/reset-test-tenant.test.ts`** (18 unit tests): isProtectedSlug refuses tenant-ritz-founder + ritz-builders + case-mismatched ritz tokens (RITZ/Ritz/RiTz-Builders/not-ritz/xritzx) + tenant-ritz- prefix variants, accepts fresh test slugs (8c9d2f4a/acme-test/test-tenant-1/friend-1), handles non-string inputs safely; parseArg (--name=value, bare --name → "true", absent → null, values with =, repeated returns first, no prefix collision); safety constants pinned (2 forbidden patterns; resettable statuses exactly pending_onboarding+active; MAX_OBSERVATIONS conservative; allowed-delete-tables exactly the 3 with negative pin on observations/snapshots/recs/changelog/entities). **NEW `tests/architecture/reset-test-tenant-contract.test.ts`** (15 invariants): file exists; exports helpers; only runs main() when invoked directly; RITZ_FORBIDDEN_PATTERNS contains the right 2 regexes; isProtectedSlug call-site runs BEFORE loadAdminClient + before any .from("tenants") query (source-order pin); allowed delete tables exactly the 3 (negative pin on others); does NOT touch auth.users (no auth.admin. / deleteUser); defaults to dry-run with `if (!confirm)` short-circuit BEFORE any .delete() call (source-order pin); prints "DRY RUN" message; refuses too_many_observations; refuses paused/cancelled (RESETTABLE_STATUSES check); no paid-API imports; no fetch/runNativePoll calls. > 🟢 **GAP F.1 — First-reading waiting state on /today (2026-05-07):** Closes the "newly launched tenant lands on /today and sees an empty/broken dashboard" UX gap immediately after Gap C.4. Surgical change: pure detector + fail-soft resolver wiring + TodayClient early-return. Mature tenants (Ritz) have observations → flag is false → /today renders byte-identical to pre-Gap-F.1. **Detection contract**: `detectFirstReadingState({tenant, activePromptCount, observationCount})` returns `isFirstReading: true` ONLY when ALL three: `tenant.status === "active"` AND `activePromptCount > 0` AND `observationCount === 0`. Defensive: NaN/negative counts → false; null tenant → false; non-active statuses → false. **NEW `src/domains/onboarding/first-reading-state.ts`** (~80 lines, pure): `detectFirstReadingState(input)` — no I/O, no env, no Date.now / Math.random / new Date; `FirstReadingContext` type `{businessName, domain, promptCount, nextReadingDescription}`; customer-safe `nextReadingDescription = "tomorrow morning"` (NEVER mentions cron/07:00 UTC/GitHub/Supabase/poll); fallbacks (blank business_name → "your business"; blank domain stays empty; trims whitespace). **NEW `src/components/today/first-reading-waiting.tsx`**: pure presentation — receives `FirstReadingContext` prop, renders headline "Beacon is preparing your first AI visibility reading.", subtitle "Your first dashboard will appear after the next daily reading.", "What's already set up" panel (business + website + prompts tracked + next reading), "What happens next" 3-step list, CTAs ("Review tracked prompts" → /prompts; "Recommendations (available after first reading)" → /recommendations); ZERO mutations / event handlers / useState / useTransition. **MODIFIED `src/app/(shell)/today-data.ts`**: imports `currentTenant` from `@/lib/tenant-context` and `detectFirstReadingState` from new domain module; private `resolveFirstReadingState(activePromptCount, observationCount)` helper wraps `currentTenant()` in try/catch (any registry-read failure logs WARN and defaults to `{isFirstReading: false}` so /today never crashes); adds `firstReading` field to `loadTodayPageData()` return object positioned after `lifecycleSummary`; counts come from `activePrompts.length` + `promptAnswerObservations.length` already loaded earlier in the resolver. **MODIFIED `src/app/(shell)/today-client.tsx`**: new import `FirstReadingWaiting`; new prop `firstReading?: FirstReadingDetection` defaulting to `{isFirstReading: false}`; **early-return immediately after the existing demo-mode early return** — if `firstReading.isFirstReading`, renders `<FirstReadingWaiting context={...} />` and skips the rest of the dashboard. Position pinned by invariant: demo → first-reading → regular dashboard (so Ritz can never accidentally hit the waiting state). **NEW `src/domains/onboarding/first-reading-state.test.ts`** (20 unit tests): happy path; non-active tenants (pending_onboarding/paused/cancelled/null) all false; missing prompts (zero/negative/NaN) false; observations exist (1 obs → false (Ritz protection); 16521 obs → false; NaN → false); Ritz-shaped regression guard (status=active + 25 prompts + 16521 obs → false); mature tenant (status=active + 10 prompts + 100 obs → false); context fallbacks (blank/whitespace business_name → "your business"; blank domain stays blank; trims); customer-safe phrasing (nextReadingDescription has no cron/UTC/GitHub/Supabase/poll/07:00; context JSON has no account_id/tenant_id/is_active/observed_at). **NEW `tests/architecture/onboard-first-reading-contract.test.ts`** (27 invariants): files exist; detector pure (no node:fs/persistence/Next.js/process.env, no Date.now/new Date/Math.random, no openai/perplexity/anthropic/fetch/runNativePoll/runWebsiteScan, exports types, no Ritz hardcoding); **trigger condition pinned at source level** (`tenant.status !== "active"`, `activePromptCount <= 0`, `observationCount > 0` regex pins so future regressions are caught); waiting component customer-safe (no cron/Supabase/GitHub/tenant/schema/poll failure/raw observations/admin/RLS/07:00/UTC; renders required headline + supporting copy + Review CTA; no mutations/paid APIs/useState/event handlers); today-data.ts wiring (imports + calls + returns firstReading + try/catch wrapped + helper body has no mutations/paid APIs); TodayClient early-return (imports component + accepts firstReading prop + renders when isFirstReading + **position pinned demo → first-reading → regular dashboard**); Ritz unchanged (detector requires observationCount > 0; lister still filters status='active'); no paid APIs / no immediate poll; read-only contract (no .from/.upsert/.insert/.update/.delete in detector; never sets status). **What still blocks customer-2 onboarding**: nothing functionally. Gap F.2 (immediate poll trigger so first reading lands minutes after Launch instead of next morning), Gap G (operator alerts on new tenant activation), Gap D (auto-detect from homepage crawl) are quality-of-life improvements. **Beacon is now friend-test ready**: signup → wizard → Launch → /today shows a confident waiting state → tomorrow morning the regular dashboard renders. Quality gates: typecheck CLEAN, full suite **335/335 files / 6177/6177 tests** (+2 files +64 tests vs Gap C.4 baseline 333/6113), build green (4 onboard routes ƒ Dynamic, /today builds clean), all 3 integrity scripts PASS (Ritz row counts UNCHANGED — confirms detector is read-only), watchdog YELLOW (queue idle WARN only), LLM budget SHA `5303c16f…` byte-identical with Gap C.4. Zero OpenAI. Zero paid polling. Zero row mutations. Zero immediate-poll triggers. > 🟢 **GAP C.4 — Launch step (the FIRST status='active' code path) (2026-05-07):** Adds the Launch surface at `/onboard/review` and the `launchTenant()` server action — the FIRST and ONLY code path that flips `tenants.status` from `pending_onboarding` to `active`. Self-serve onboarding is now end-to-end functional. **Highest-safety mini-phase to date** — full preflight on schema + atomicity options before code, app-layer dedup, double-click-safe lock-out, explicit rollback on flip failure, 23 behavioral tests against in-memory Supabase mock, 34 architecture invariants. **Preflight findings**: tracked_prompts uses `account_id` (= tenants.slug) scoping, no unique index on (account_id, text), no Postgres RPCs in codebase, supabase-js doesn't natively support multi-statement transactions. Three atomicity options considered: (A) new RPC migration — rejected (adds infrastructure for one use case); (B) **insert prompts BEFORE flip + rollback DELETE on flip error — chosen** (keeps "active tenant always has prompts" contract); (C) insert as is_active=false then atomic flip both — rejected (still 2 separate calls without transaction support, worse failure mode). **NEW `src/app/(shell)/onboard/review/launch-flow.ts`** (~210 lines, pure-injected helpers, NOT `"use server"` so test fixtures can import sync helpers): `LaunchTransactionOutcome` type (`redirect` with reason `success`/`already_launched`/`race_lost`, or `error`); `buildTrackedPromptRow({draft, accountId, now, id?})` pure mapper from PromptDraft → tracked_prompts row (defaults `intent_type="recommendation"`, `platforms=["perplexity","chatgpt"]`, `tags=["starter_v0", cluster, "priority-N"]`, `is_active=true`, auto-generates `prompt-${randomUUID()}`); `executeLaunchTransaction({admin, tenantId, now})` does the full flow with injected deps — fetches tenant, validates status, regenerates prompts server-side, dedups against existing tracked_prompts.text (case-insensitive), bulk-INSERTs new prompts (is_active=true), then UPDATEs tenants with double-click guard, on UPDATE error DELETEs inserted prompts (rollback), on UPDATE 0-rows keeps prompts (race-won by another path). **NEW `src/app/(shell)/onboard/review/actions.ts`** (`"use server"`, ~95 lines): thin `launchTenant({tosAccepted})` wrapper validates TOS, resolves user from session, looks up tenant via lookupExistingMembership, delegates to executeLaunchTransaction, translates `redirect` outcome into Next.js `redirect()` call. **NEW `src/app/(shell)/onboard/review/launch-form.tsx`** (client): TOS checkbox with pill-style state; "Launch Beacon" button disabled until TOS accepted AND promptCount > 0; submits via useTransition + launchTenant; calls action with ONLY `{tosAccepted}` (never sends prompt list from client); NEXT_REDIRECT propagation; humanized error codes. **MODIFIED `src/app/(shell)/onboard/review/page.tsx`**: subtitle changed to "Beacon will start tracking these prompts on the next daily reading."; added `<LaunchForm promptCount={drafts.length}>` between preview list and Back link; page itself still does NOT mutate (writes go through action). **ATOMICITY CONTRACT (the safety core)**: order is fetch → validate → regenerate prompts → dedup → INSERT prompts → UPDATE tenants. If UPDATE returns 1 row → success → redirect. If UPDATE errors → DELETE prompts by id (rollback). If UPDATE returns 0 rows → race lost or already activated → keep prompts (legitimate) → redirect. The "active tenant with zero prompts" failure mode is impossible: UPDATE only succeeds if INSERT already succeeded. **DOUBLE-CLICK SAFETY**: `UPDATE WHERE status='pending_onboarding' AND tos_accepted_at IS NULL`. After first call sets tos_accepted_at, second call's WHERE excludes the row → 0 rows → redirect. Prompt-level dedup via SELECT-then-filter prevents most duplicates; rare race producing duplicate-text rows still polls correctly. **NEW `src/app/(shell)/onboard/review/launch-flow.test.ts`** (23 behavioral tests with in-memory Supabase mock): `buildTrackedPromptRow` field mapping + defaults + auto-id; happy path (insert before update, tenant active with TOS+updated_at, all 4 generator families); dedup (case-insensitive, resumed-launch with all pre-existing prompts); already-launched (no inserts); race-lost (no rollback); validation errors (no_prompts_generated, paused, cancelled, missing); failure rollback (insert fails → no state change; UPDATE fails → rollback DELETE; tenant fetch fails; existing-prompts fetch fails); tenant isolation (Ritz prompts untouched, rollback DELETE only this tenant's prompts via id IN + account_id filter). **NEW `tests/architecture/onboard-launch-step-contract.test.ts`** (34 invariants): files exist; force-dynamic + access guard; LaunchForm renders; UX (button disabled until TOS, no payment/passwords/Stripe, no tenant/admin/RLS/schema/Supabase/cron/GitHub language); action `"use server"`, resolves user from session, takes only `{tosAccepted}` input; flow helper regenerates prompts server-side, returns no_prompts_generated on empty; UPDATE has double-click guard (`status='pending_onboarding'` AND `tos_accepted_at IS NULL`); UPDATE flips status='active' + tos_accepted_at + updated_at; **the activating UPDATE is the ONLY place `status:"active"` appears in launch sources** (combined wrapper + helper); INSERT runs BEFORE UPDATE; rollback DELETE filters by both id IN (...) AND account_id; account_id sourced from tenant.slug; is_active=true on insert; no paid APIs / no immediate poll / no other table writes; redirect target only /today; cross-check (lister still filters status='active'; nothing in C.1/C.2/C.3 sets status='active'; Ritz prompts pinned via account_id scoping). **MODIFIED prior tests**: E.1's "Beacon will start by tracking" copy → "Beacon will start tracking these prompts" (Gap C.4 changed copy); E.1's "preview-only / no Launch button" → "page itself is read-only (writes go through Launch action)"; C.3 cross-page sweep PUBLIC_FACING_PAGES updated to include launch-form.tsx. **What still blocks customer-2 onboarding**: nothing functionally. Gap D (auto-detect from homepage), Gap F (day-0 first-poll trigger / waiting state), Gap G (operator alerts on new tenant activation) are quality-of-life. Quality gates: typecheck CLEAN, full suite **333/333 files / 6113/6113 tests** (+2 files +61 tests vs Gap E.1 baseline 331/6052), build green (4 onboard routes ƒ Dynamic), all 3 integrity scripts PASS (Ritz row counts UNCHANGED — confirms zero mutations during code changes), watchdog YELLOW (queue idle WARN only), LLM budget SHA `5303c16f…` byte-identical with Gap E.1. Zero OpenAI. Zero paid polling. Zero row mutations to active tenants during implementation. No second active tenant. Zero immediate-poll triggers. Details: [`docs/VERIFICATION_LOG.md`](VERIFICATION_LOG.md#2026-05-07--self-serve-onboarding-gap-c4-launch-step-the-first-statusactive-code-path).

> 🟢 **GAP E.1 — Deterministic prompt generator v0 + /onboard/review preview (2026-05-07):** Adds deterministic, template-only starter-prompt generator; `/onboard/review` now shows up to 25 generated prompts grouped by family (brand discovery / competitor comparison / service-in-city / cost queries). **Preview-only persistence** — no DB write in E.1; Gap C.4 Launch will persist + flip status atomically. Continues the "block C.4 Launch on Gap E so an active tenant always has prompts" rule. **Preflight findings**: cron path is `daily-native-poll.yml` → Gap A's `list-active-tenants.ts` (filters `tenants.status='active'`) → matrix dispatch → adapter `.filter(p => p.is_active)`. `tracked_prompts` schema gating column is `is_active: boolean` only (no `is_draft`/`status`). Two safe paths: (A) preview-only, defer DB write to C.4 — chosen; (B) write rows with `is_active=false` — rejected to keep new write surface at zero. **NEW `src/domains/onboarding/prompt-generator.ts`** (~190 lines, pure): `generateStarterPrompts({businessName, domain, citiesServed, projectMix, competitors})` returns up to 25 PromptDraft[] across 4 priority-ordered families: 1. brand discovery (2 prompts: "{name} reviews", "is {name} a good company"); 2. competitor comparison ≤5 ("{name} vs {competitor}"); 3. service-in-city primary (1 per city × service: "best {service} in {city}"); 4. service-in-city alternates (3 phrasings × city × service: "top {service} companies in {city}", "who should I hire for a {service} in {city}", "best company for {service} near {city}"); 5. cost / long-tail (1 per service, anchored on first city: "how much does a {service} cost in {city}"). `STARTER_PROMPT_MAX_COUNT = 25` (operator-locked). `PromptCategory` union pinning exactly 4 families. `SERVICE_PROMPT_TERMS: Record<ProjectMixTag, string>` (homeowner-natural phrasings: new_construction → "custom home builder", whole_home_remodel → "home remodel", kitchen_bath → "kitchen remodel", adu_addition → "ADU builder", teardown_rebuild → "teardown and rebuild", commercial_residential → "design-build contractor"). `Record<...>` type pin = build-time error if ProjectMixTag drifts. `stripStateAbbreviation` strips ", ST" from prompt text but preserves original in `city_scope` field. **PromptDraft shape** (compatible with future tracked_prompts insert): `{ text, cluster, city_scope, service_scope, category, priority, rationale }`. **Determinism**: no Math.random / Date.now / new Date / env / I/O. Same input → same output. JSON-serializable. **REPLACED `src/app/(shell)/onboard/review/page.tsx`**: was C.3 placeholder (saved-so-far summary only), now server component (force-dynamic) calling `requireOnboardingTenant()` + `generateStarterPrompts(...)` with saved tenant inputs; renders saved summary AND generated preview grouped by family with operator-readable category labels ("Brand searches", "Head-to-head with competitors", "City + service queries", "Cost questions"); "Beacon will start by tracking these prompts" copy. **Still no Launch button** (Gap C.4 owns Launch). **No DB write.** Empty-state ("No starter prompts yet") shown when generator returns 0. **NEW `src/domains/onboarding/prompt-generator.test.ts`** (30 unit tests): cap + dedupe (≤25, case-insensitive, stable 1..N priority); determinism (same input → same output, JSON-serializable); uses every input axis (businessName in brand; competitors in comp; cities in city_scope; every service tag in service_scope; ", CA" stripped from text but preserved in city_scope); sparse inputs (1+1+1, 0 competitors, 0 cities, 0 services, 0 of everything → [], only business name → 2 brand prompts); defense (ignores unknown ProjectMixTag, blank/non-string array entries, trims whitespace, never empty text); no Ritz hardcoding (Ritz/ritzbuilders/ritz-builders/Atherton-93022/Beacon never appear); customer-safe rationale (no tenant/admin/RLS/schema/Supabase/cron/GitHub leak); distribution at saturation (brand 2 + competitor 5 always preserved, service-in-city saturates remainder); output shape (every field has correct type; brand + comp have null scopes; service_in_city + cost_query have BOTH city + service scope; cluster === category in v0). **NEW `tests/architecture/onboard-prompt-generator-contract.test.ts`** (24 invariants): generator file exists; exports generateStarterPrompts + STARTER_PROMPT_MAX_COUNT=25 + types; pure module (no node:fs/node:path/getSupabase/next/process.env/Math.random/Date.now/new Date); no paid APIs (openai/perplexity/anthropic/google/fetch/XMLHttpRequest/axios/runNativePoll/runWebsiteScan/syncRecommendedEdits); no Ritz hardcoding after comment-strip (no Ritz tokens, no known competitor names as string literals, no Ritz cities as string literals); /onboard/review imports + calls generateStarterPrompts with saved tenant inputs from tenant.* fields; "Beacon will start by tracking" copy rendered; **/onboard/review does NOT write to tracked_prompts (preview-only contract)**; no upsert/insert/update/delete; no paid APIs; no Launch `<button>`; no status:"active"; no save action calls; cross-check: list-active-tenants.ts still filters status='active' (Gap A intact); /onboard/review never inserts into tracked_prompts; prompt-generator never writes anywhere; cap pinned at 25; family contract pinned at exactly 4 categories; SERVICE_PROMPT_TERMS is `Record<ProjectMixTag, string>` (build-time pin); customer-safe sweep on /onboard/review (no tenant/admin/RLS/schema/Supabase/cron/GitHub). **What still blocks full self-serve**: Gap C.4 (Launch step — flips pending_onboarding → active, writes prompts as is_active=true in single transaction, sets tos_accepted_at), auto-detect (Gap D), Day-0 first-poll trigger (Gap F), operator alerts (Gap G). Direct-row persistence (no separate `tenant_drafts` table needed — pending tenants are already isolated from cron). Every write is gated by `WHERE id = ? AND status = 'pending_onboarding'` so an active customer's row can never be mutated through this path. **No tenant activation. No prompt creation. No paid APIs.** **NEW `src/domains/onboarding/profile-validation.ts`** (~110 lines): pure validators — `normalizeDomain` (strip http/https scheme + leading // + paths + query + fragments + whitespace + user:pass@ + :port; lowercase; RFC-1123 label validation; require ≥1 dot; require letter in TLD; reject `javascript:`/`data:`/`mailto:` schemes; reject bare IPs; type-safe vs undefined/null/non-string), `validateBusinessProfile` (trim+empty+80-char-cap on name, normalize domain). **NEW `src/domains/onboarding/access.ts`** (~80 lines, server-only): `requireOnboardingTenant()` access guard with full redirect matrix — no user → /login?next=/onboard/business; lookup error → /login?error=onboarding_lookup_failed; no membership → /signup?error=no_tenant; tenant fetch error → /login?error=tenant_fetch_failed; tenant missing → /login?error=tenant_missing; status=active → / (already launched); status=paused → /?error=tenant_paused; status=cancelled → /?error=tenant_cancelled; only `pending_onboarding` falls through. Read-only. **NEW `src/components/onboard/onboarding-shell.tsx`**: shared centered card layout matching /signup styling; renders 4-step indicator with `aria-current="step"` + ARIA label `Step N of 4: <label>`; exports `ONBOARDING_TOTAL_STEPS = 4`. **REPLACED `src/app/(shell)/onboard/business/page.tsx`**: was Gap B placeholder welcome screen, now server component (force-dynamic) calling `requireOnboardingTenant()` + rendering `<OnboardingShell step={1} title="Set up your business">` with `<BusinessForm>`; pre-fills saved business_name + domain but suppresses the auto-derived `"New Beacon Account"` placeholder so operator types real input. **NEW `src/app/(shell)/onboard/business/business-form.tsx`** (client): two inputs (businessName autocomplete=organization, domain autocomplete=url + inputMode=url); no password/payment/Stripe/CVV; submits via `useTransition` + server action; per-field error rendering + Next.js redirect signal re-throw; humanizes server error codes for UI. **NEW `src/app/(shell)/onboard/business/actions.ts`** (`"use server"`): `saveBusinessProfile({businessName, domain})` — validates pure, resolves user from session cookie, looks up tenant via `lookupExistingMembership`, then `UPDATE tenants SET business_name, domain, updated_at WHERE id = ? AND status = 'pending_onboarding'` (count: 'exact'); on `count === 0` returns `already_launched`; on success calls `redirect('/onboard/scope')`. Touches ONLY the `tenants` table; no tracked_prompts/tracked_entities/observations/recommended_edits/snapshots; no openai/perplexity/runNativePoll/runWebsiteScan/acceptAllHighConfidence/syncRecommendedEdits. **NEW `src/app/(shell)/onboard/scope/page.tsx`**: step-2 placeholder; same access guard; force-dynamic; renders saved business + domain (echo from step 1) + "Coming up" preview list. ZERO inputs/forms/selects/textareas. **NEW `src/domains/onboarding/profile-validation.test.ts`** (28 unit tests): scheme stripping; www preservation; path/query/fragment stripping; lowercase; whitespace trim; port + user:pass@ stripping; multi-label TLDs; single-label rejection; javascript:/data:/mailto: XSS hardening; bare IP rejection; invalid label characters; non-string safety; business profile validate happy/empty/whitespace/80-char boundary/malformed-domain/javascript-domain/both-fields-invalid/undefined-keys-safe. **NEW `tests/architecture/onboard-business-step-contract.test.ts`** (35 invariants): page + form + action exist; force-dynamic; uses requireOnboardingTenant + OnboardingShell step={1}; form has EXACTLY 2 inputs (businessName, domain); no payment/passwords/Stripe/CVV in form; no tenant/admin/RLS/schema language in customer-rendered text; saveBusinessProfile uses `"use server"` + validateBusinessProfile + getSupabaseAdmin; UPDATE gated by `.eq("id", ...).eq("status", "pending_onboarding")`; never sets `status: "active"`; touches ONLY tenants table; redirects to /onboard/scope on success; returns validation_failed on bad input; surfaces already_launched on race; /onboard/scope is force-dynamic + step={2} + ZERO mutations + ZERO inputs; access guard is server-only + read-only + has full redirect matrix; OnboardingShell renders step indicator with constant 4; cross-check: list-active-tenants.ts still filters status='active' AND nothing in /onboard/* code flips status to 'active'. **MODIFIED `tests/architecture/signup-onboarding-routes-contract.test.ts`**: dropped Gap B welcome-copy assertions (superseded by Gap C.1 form contract); kept route-level invariants (file exists, force-dynamic, page itself doesn't mutate / call paid APIs, lives under (shell) group). **What still blocks full self-serve**: /onboard/scope form (Gap C.2 — cities + services), /onboard/competitors (Gap C.3), /onboard/review + Launch (Gap C.4 — flips status pending_onboarding → active), auto-detect (Gap D), auto-prompts (Gap E). > 🟢 **GAP B — Public /signup + auth-callback tenant provisioning (2026-05-07):** Second step in customer-onboarding pipeline (Gap A → Gap B). Customers can now sign themselves up via magic-link at `/signup`, get a `pending_onboarding` tenant + `tenant_members` row provisioned automatically on first auth-callback, and land on a placeholder `/onboard/business` page. Cron pipeline ignores them (Gap A's lister filters `status='active'`) — operator must explicitly flip `tenants.status` to `active` before daily polling starts. **Reversible**: pending tenants don't get polled; new status value can be removed from CHECK constraint once no rows hold it; signup is one middleware revert away. **NEW `src/domains/onboarding/provision-tenant.ts`** (~165 lines): `PROVISIONING_DEFAULTS` (status=pending_onboarding, role=beta_customer, daily_budget_usd=5, member_role=owner) — operator-locked, pinned against accidental flip to `'active'`; `deriveTenantId(userId)` (deterministic `tenant-${first8hex}`); `derivePlaceholderBusinessName(email)` (title-cased domain prefix, fallback "New Beacon Account"); `lookupExistingMembership(supabase, userId)` (pure read); `provisionTenantForNewUser(supabase, input, now?)` (idempotent: lookup → if exists return `created:false` → else upsert tenants ignoreDuplicates → upsert tenant_members → return `created:true`; structured `phase: "lookup"|"tenant_insert"|"member_insert"` on failure). **NEW `src/app/(public)/signup/{page,signup-form,actions}.tsx`**: server component (force-dynamic, branded "Create your Beacon account", customer-safe — no tenant/admin/RLS/schema language in rendered HTML); client form (email-only, `signInWithOtp` with `shouldCreateUser:true`, `emailRedirectTo:${origin}/auth/callback?signup=1`); already-authenticated users redirect to `/`; no password / no Stripe / no payment. **Modified `src/app/auth/callback/route.ts`**: after exchangeCodeForSession, calls `provisionTenantForNewUser(admin, ...)` using `getSupabaseAdmin()` (service-role bypass of RLS deny-all on tenants); first-time → `/onboard/business`; repeat sign-in → caller's `next` or `/`; failure → `/signup?error=provisioning_<phase>` (idempotent inserts heal orphans on next attempt). **NEW `src/app/(shell)/onboard/business/page.tsx`**: placeholder welcome page (force-dynamic, customer-safe "Welcome to Beacon" + 4-step "Coming up" preview), reads tenant via `lookupExistingMembership` best-effort (page survives Supabase blips), operator-debug surface shows tenant slug+status (will be removed when Gap C wizard ships). **Modified `src/lib/auth/supabase-middleware.ts`**: added `path.startsWith("/signup")` to `isPublic` predicate (line 86) — `/auth` and `/login` predicates preserved. **Modified `src/domains/tenants/types.ts`**: extended `BeaconTenant.status` with `"pending_onboarding"`. **DB migration applied via Supabase MCP**: `ALTER TABLE public.tenants` extending `tenants_status_check` to allow `pending_onboarding` alongside active|paused|cancelled (verified post-migration via `pg_get_constraintdef`). **NEW `src/domains/onboarding/provision-tenant.test.ts`** (28 behavioral tests with in-memory mock Supabase — no network, no env vars): pure helpers (`deriveTenantId` deterministic+idempotent+UUID-case-insensitive, `derivePlaceholderBusinessName` title-cases+splits+fallback, `PROVISIONING_DEFAULTS` operator-locked); `lookupExistingMembership` (null/hit/error); happy path (creates tenant+member rows with all defaults, owner role); idempotency (repeat call no dup writes; existing membership returns existing tenantId without touching tenants; orphan tenant → second call completes missing membership); failure modes (lookup/tenant_insert/member_insert each surfacing structured phase). **NEW `tests/architecture/signup-onboarding-routes-contract.test.ts`** (21 invariants): /signup is dynamic + customer-safe + magic-link; auth callback uses admin client + provisioner + correct redirects; /onboard/business is dynamic + no paid APIs/mutations + (shell) group; middleware exposes /signup; provisioner uses pending_onboarding (not active) + ignoreDuplicates + no paid APIs; cross-check Gap A's lister filters status='active' so pending excluded from cron picking. **What still blocks full self-serve**: onboarding wizard (Gap C — replace placeholder with 4-step business profile collection), auto-detect engine (Gap D), auto-prompt generator (Gap E), waiting state (Gap F), operator alerts (Gap G). Verification: anon REST `GET /rest/v1/tracked_prompts` returns `[]` (RLS filtered all rows); anon REST `POST /rest/v1/recommended_edits` returns `HTTP 401` + Postgres `42501` ("new row violates row-level security policy"); service-role canary + repository facade reads return expected counts (100 prompts / 40 entities / 28 recs / 334 changelog / 199 obs for May 6); typecheck clean; full suite 4669/4674 (5 baseline failures unchanged); `npm run build` green; ledger byte-identical (SHA `d36eed8c…`); hosted sign-in smoke confirmed — no `/login?error=no_tenant` loop. Security Advisor now reports **0 ERRORs**. **One WARN remains: `auth_leaked_password_protection`. Acknowledged and intentionally deferred** — Beacon currently uses magic-link / OTP auth only (`signInWithOtp` is the sole auth call across `src/app/login/`, `src/app/auth/`, and `src/lib/auth/`). Leaked-password protection only fires on `signInWithPassword` and password-creation/update flows, of which Beacon has zero. The toggle also requires Supabase **Pro Plan** (current org plan: `free`). **Precondition for any future password-based sign-up / sign-in:** (1) upgrade Supabase to Pro; (2) enable leaked-password protection in dashboard → Authentication → Sign In/Up; (3) verify password flows under the toggle. Magic-link auth is unaffected by deferral. Docs/details: [`docs/VERIFICATION_LOG.md`](VERIFICATION_LOG.md#2026-05-06--rls-deny-all-migration--leaked-password-protection-decision); customer-2 onboarding precondition added in [`docs/NEXT_PHASE_EXECUTION_PLAN.md`](NEXT_PHASE_EXECUTION_PLAN.md). > 🟢 **CUSTOMER-2 ISOLATION FIX — TRACKED_PROMPTS + TRACKED_ENTITIES TENANT-SCOPED (2026-05-06):** Operator-approved bundle (item 1 of 4 split) closed the customer-2 onboarding data-leak path. Pre-fix: `loadFreshCanonicalData()` + Supabase-merge branch of `loadFromDiskAndMerge` called the unscoped `repo.getTrackedPrompts()` / `repo.getTrackedEntities()` — Tier-A-scoped + Tier-C-unscoped — which would have silently mixed Ritz's prompts + competitors into customer-2's /today leaderboard at onboarding time. Post-fix: both reads route through `tenantRepo.forTenant(tenantId)`. **TenantRepository interface extended** with `getTrackedPrompts()` / `getTrackedEntities()`. **File backend** filters in-memory via `filterByTenantId` (rows on disk carry both `tenant_id` + `account_id`). **Supabase backend** resolves the slug via `getTenant(tenantId)`, returns `[]` for unknown tenants, filters with `.eq("account_id", tenant.slug)` against tables whose schema (verified live) uses `account_id text NOT NULL`, NOT `tenant_id`. **No Supabase migration required.** NEW invariant `tests/architecture/canonical-store-tenant-isolation.test.ts` (13/13 PASS) pins source-text shape in 3 blocks: canonical-store uses tenant-scoped reads, interface declares both methods, both backends implement them correctly. NEW behavioral test `tests/persistence/tenant-isolation-behavioral.test.ts` (6/6 PASS) builds a fake repo with mixed-tenant rows and proves: populated tenant gets only its rows, empty tenant gets `[]`, two populated tenants are mutually disjoint by id-set, AND Ritz regression (tenant-ritz-founder loads its full data without cross-tenant leak). UPDATED `tests/persistence/tenant-repository-pushdown.test.ts` accepts `.eq("account_id", tenant.slug)` as a documented exception. FLIPPED `tests/routes/canonical-store-fresh.test.ts` — pre-2026-05-06 invariant pinned the OLD bug ("Tier C reads stay unscoped"); inverted to assert tenant-scoped reads + no raw `repo.getTracked*` in the canonical fresh-load source. Quality gate: typecheck baseline (3 pre-existing prompt-drilldown errors), full suite 4669/4674 (same 5 baseline failures unchanged; +19 new passing tests vs Profound-readiness baseline of 4650), build EXIT_CODE=0 green, ledger byte-identical (SHA `d36eed8c…`). **Next bundles in the 4-item split (onboarding scaffold + cron + remaining items) are unblocked.** Report: [`docs/VERIFICATION_LOG.md`](VERIFICATION_LOG.md#2026-05-06--customer-2-isolation-fix-tracked_prompts--tracked_entities-tenant-scoped).

> 🟢 **E1-E6 SUPABASE EGRESS AUDIT + OPTIMIZATION (2026-05-05):** Audited all Supabase reads from product routes + scripts + cron. Found root cause: `/today` + `/prompts` + `/diagnostics` paged the FULL ~14k-row `prompt_answer_observations` table on every render via `loadFreshCanonicalData()` — ~42 MB egress per page load, multiplied by dev refreshes + cold-start prerenders. Landed: (a) windowed-read API (`since` option) on `getPromptAnswerObservations` + `getDailyMetricSnapshots`, (b) `loadFreshCanonicalData({ observationsSince, snapshotsSince })`, (c) `/today` uses 60-day obs + 120-day snapshot windows, (d) `/prompts` uses 60-day obs window, (e) `page_snapshots` capped at LIMIT 5000, (f) egress instrumentation behind `BEACON_SUPABASE_EGRESS_DEBUG=1`, (g) architecture invariant pinning `/diagnostics` doesn't pull observations directly. **Expected egress reduction: 60-90% on /today + /prompts page loads** (the dominant cost). No paid calls, no schema changes.
>
> ## E6 Decision Report
>
> ### 1. Root cause estimate (highest → lowest)
>
> | Source | Est. share | Why |
> |---|---|---|
> | **`/today` + `/prompts` rendering** | ~60-70% | Each page load called `loadFreshCanonicalData()` which paged ALL observations + snapshots tenant-wide. ~14k obs × ~3KB/row = ~42 MB observations + ~6 MB snapshots = ~48 MB per render. Dev refreshes (operator + agent loops) compound this 5-20× per day. |
> | **URL-watcher trigger on /today + /changes** | ~10-15% | `url-citation-history.ts` re-loads all observations every 6h via `getPromptAnswerObservations()`. Dev refreshes bypass the throttle. |
> | **Repeat dev browsing** | ~10% | Each Vercel preview / local dev page hit re-cold-starts the canonical store and re-pulls everything. |
> | **Verify-daily-poll runs** | ~5-10% | 5-day observation lookback to count by platform — should use `count: 'exact', head: true` per (date, platform). Modest payload (~30KB/run × 3 runs/day) but adds up. |
> | **Citation index rebuild post-cron** | ~5% | Pulls all native observations to rebuild the index. Once per day; runs cleanly. |
> | **Stage 7 / W4 backfill repeat reads** | (negligible) | Backfill pre-publishes locally; published once on 2026-05-04. |
>
> ### 2. Expected egress reduction (after E1-E5 land)
>
> | Path | Pre-E5 payload (per render) | Post-E5 payload | Reduction |
> |---|---|---|---|
> | `/today` cold start | ~48 MB (14k obs + 7k snaps, full select-*) | ~10-12 MB (60d obs ≈ 1k rows + 120d snaps ≈ 720 rows) | **~75% drop** |
> | `/prompts` cold start | ~42 MB (full obs) | ~3-5 MB (60d obs ≈ 1k rows) | **~88% drop** |
> | `/diagnostics` cold start | ~10 MB (no obs, but other reads) | unchanged ~10 MB (architecture invariant prevents accidental obs leak) | 0% (was already light) |
> | `getPageSnapshots` | unbounded (could be 15-20 MB on a tenant with 5000+ snapshots) | capped at 5000 rows ≈ 15 MB max | bounded |
> | URL-watcher pass | ~7 MB (full obs) | ~7 MB (unchanged — backfill scripts intentionally still load all) | 0% (deferred — see "Next 3" below) |
>
> **Daily egress projection** (single dev visiting /today + /prompts ~10× per day): pre-E5 ~960 MB/day worst-case = ~29 GB/month → post-E5 ~150-180 MB/day = ~5 GB/month. **Comfortably under the Free Plan 5 GB cap.** Customer-1 (Ritz) production cron egress is bounded; the dev-refresh storm was the dominant variable.
>
> ### 3. Plan recommendation: **Option C — wait until May 9 reset, monitor for one cycle**
>
> Don't upgrade Supabase to Pro yet. Reasoning:
> - Most of the 5.7 GB this cycle was wasteful, not real customer load (1 MAU, 0 storage egress, 0 realtime, 0 edge).
> - The 60-day window optimization should drop /today + /prompts egress by ~75-88% per render.
> - Dev refresh storms on the new windowed paths cost ~10-15 MB each (vs ~50 MB pre-E5) — easy to stay under 5 GB/month.
> - Customer-2 isn't onboarded; daily cron load is bounded.
>
> **Monitor for one Free Plan cycle (May 9 → June 8).** If we approach 4 GB by mid-cycle with windowing in place, that's a real signal — upgrade then. Until then: stay Free.
>
> **If/when Pro is recommended later**, the reason will be: daily native polling on multiple tenants + dashboard reads from a customer base genuinely need it. Not because we're wasting egress.
>
> ### 4. Top-5 egress offenders + exact fixes landed
>
> | Rank | Offender | Fix landed (commit at end) |
> |---|---|---|
> | **1** | `loadFreshCanonicalData()` paged ALL `prompt_answer_observations` on /today, /prompts | New `FreshCanonicalDataOptions { observationsSince, snapshotsSince }`. /today passes 60d obs + 120d snaps. /prompts passes 60d obs. The `since` filter pushes down to Postgres `.gte("observed_at", since)`. **~75-88% reduction per render.** |
> | **2** | `loadFreshCanonicalData()` also paged ALL `daily_metric_snapshots` | Same window applied. 7k → ~720 rows × 0.8KB = ~5MB → ~600KB. |
> | **3** | `getPageSnapshots` was unbounded (`.select("*").order(...)` then dedupe-in-JS) | Added `.limit(5000)`. The dedupe-by-page_id still gets the latest per page; old rows past the cap are dropped (acceptable for current tenant scale). Egress logged via the new instrumentation. |
> | **4** | No instrumentation — couldn't measure offenders without redeploy | New `logEgress()` helper in `supabase-backend.ts`. Wraps `query`, `queryAllPaged`, `selectScoped`, `queryAllPagedScoped`, plus `getPageSnapshots`. Activates only when `BEACON_SUPABASE_EGRESS_DEBUG=1` (off in prod by default). Logs table, rows, ≈bytes, ms, tenant, filter. |
> | **5** | `/diagnostics` could regress and start pulling observations directly | Architecture invariant `tests/architecture/supabase-egress-windowing.test.ts` pins that `/diagnostics` does NOT call `loadFreshCanonicalData()` and does NOT call `.getPromptAnswerObservations()`. CI fails loudly if a future commit accidentally adds a heavy read. |
>
> ### Constraints respected
>
> - No paid OpenAI calls. No paid generation. No backfill. No extra polls.
> - No schema migrations. No data deletion.
> - No UI redesign. No onboarding kickoff. No Profound archive.
> - File-backend symmetry preserved: `tenant-repo` honors `{ since }` via in-memory filter so `DATA_SOURCE=file` works identically.
> - Default behavior unchanged when `since` is omitted — scripts (verify-daily-poll, citation index rebuild, URL watcher, materializer) keep loading full history.
>
> ### Next 3 actions
>
> 1. **Watch the next 24-48h Vercel egress** — if /today + /prompts page loads drop the daily Supabase egress trend by 60%+, the optimization landed. If not, the URL-watcher pass (or another path I missed) is the dominant remaining cost; trace via `BEACON_SUPABASE_EGRESS_DEBUG=1` on a Vercel preview.
> 2. **Decide URL-watcher window cutoff** — the watcher reloads ALL observations on every materialize pass to compute URL citation history. It legitimately needs broader history than /today's 60 days (the visibility chart goes back to native-regime-start = 2026-04-22, ~14 days). A future bundle could window it to 90 days too — saves ~40 MB per pass × 2-4 passes per day.
> 3. **At Free Plan reset (May 9)** — recheck egress trend. If still > 4 GB after one full cycle of windowed reads, escalate. Otherwise keep Free until customer-2.

> 🟢 **S1+S3+S4 TRUST/INFRA BUNDLE (2026-05-05):** Measurement quality boundary pinned + tenant-isolation baseline failure fixed + samplingStatus guard fully wired into URL-level attribution. **Full suite improved 6 → 5 baseline failures (tenant-isolation now passes).** typecheck clean, 72/72 targeted PASS (S1 + S3 + S4 + url-verdict), full suite 4311/4316 (+29 passing vs M5), build green.
>
> ## What S1 did (pin measurement quality boundary)
>
> - **Single canonical constant**: `NATIVE_REGIME_START = "2026-04-22"` exported from `src/domains/product/url-citation-history.ts`. Audited the entire `src/` tree: only one declaration, and the architecture invariant blocks any future literal duplicate.
> - **Architecture invariant** `tests/architecture/measurement-quality-boundary-pin.test.ts` pins three contracts: (1) the canonical export exists with value `2026-04-22`; (2) the canonical file declares it exactly once; (3) no other src/** file (excluding test fixtures) declares a `2026-04-22` date literal.
> - **Mixed-source attribution test** in the same invariant: `denseSeries` correctly tags pre-boundary points as `benchmark` and post-boundary points as `derived`; pure-split benchmark→derived windows abstain with `not_enough_native_baseline` (the load-bearing guard). 5/5 PASS.
> - **No rename**: operator brief said "Rename only if safe. Do not do broad refactors." `MEASUREMENT_QUALITY_BOUNDARY` rename would touch 27+ call sites across 6 domains — left for a dedicated pass.
>
> ## What S3 did (fix tenant-isolation baseline failure)
>
> - **Root cause**: `.data/observation-runs.json` had 50 rows with no `tenant_id` field — they predate the multi-tenant migration that backfilled every other store. `filterByTenant` strict-equals on `tenant_id`, so all 50 rows got filtered out, returning 0 for any tenant query (including `tenant-ritz-founder`).
> - **Fix path**: read-time normalization in `src/lib/tenant-data.ts`. New `getFounderTenantIdForLegacyFallback()` resolves the founder tenant once (cached per process) by `role === "founder"` from the tenant store. New `filterByTenantWithLegacyFounderFallback` interprets untagged rows as belonging to the founder, returns shallow copies with the canonical `tenant_id` stamped on. **No on-disk mutation. No Ritz-specific assumption** (resolves dynamically from the tenant registry).
> - **Cross-tenant safety**: queries for any non-founder tenant still return 0 untagged rows (the legacy fallback only matches the founder query).
> - **Result**: full tenant-isolation suite now 18/18 PASS (was 17/18). Full beacon suite 5 baseline failures (down from 6).
>
> ## What S4 did (samplingStatus guard in attribution)
>
> - **Wire-up**: M3 added the guard in `computeUrlVerdict` (demote helping/hurting → nothing_yet when post-window has any proof day OR no full days), but the guard was a no-op because no caller populated `sampling_status` on `DailyPoint`. S4 fills the gap.
> - **New helpers** in `src/domains/attribution/url-change-outcome.ts`:
>   - `buildSamplingStatusByDate(observations)` — counts `prompt_answer_observations` per ISO date and classifies each via `classifySampling()` (full ≥ 80, partial 10–79, proof 1–9, empty 0).
>   - `stampSamplingStatus(series, map)` — pure shallow-copy stamper. Untagged dates left as-is (back-compat).
> - **`computeChangeVerdict`** accepts new optional `samplingStatusByDate` in `ComputeVerdictOptions`. Stamps the dense series after `denseSeries` returns; engine guard fires.
> - **`materializeUrlOutcomes`** builds the map once per pass from `getPromptAnswerObservations()`. Failure to load is non-fatal — falls back to empty map (untagged points; no-op guard) with a `log.warn`.
> - **13 new tests** in `src/domains/attribution/url-change-outcome.s4-sampling.test.ts`:
>   - Threshold classification (full / partial / proof / empty boundary cases).
>   - Untagged-date and empty-input pass-through.
>   - End-to-end: proof day in post-window demotes; partial-only post demotes; full-only post keeps helping; historical_recovered FULL samples (pre-boundary dates) still produce wins (status-based not date-based); back-compat (no map → engine unchanged).
>   - Stamped-tag shape check on returned series.
>
